## MC_011-013 (Dec63-Apr64)
- Sun-dasa marriage: Sun connected to 2/7/11 (occupy / in star of occupants or lords of 2/7/11) gives marriage in Sun dasa, in sub of planet connected to 2/7/11. Same for Moon dasa (MC_012). Consistent w/ R4.
- Profession: planet connected to 2/6/10 defined as (a) occupy 2/6/10, (b) in star of occupant of 2/6/10, (c) owner, (d) in star of owner, (e) conjoined with lords, (f) aspected by lords, (g) aspected by planet connected. (MC_012 Moon period)
- Nehru chart (Jan 1964): 14-11-1889 11:27:30 PM LMT, 25°26'N 81°52'E, ayanamsa 22°13', Nirayana Asc Cancer 28°22', MC Aries 26°09'. Rahu dasa Mer bhukti from 12-11-1962. => TEST CASE + ayanamsa check (22°13' in 1889 consistent with 23°18' in 1967 @ ~50"/yr).
- Ganapathy erection (Apr 1964): 15-5-1963 6:30 PM IST Agra, ayanamsa 23°15' (1963). Moon Sravanam 3, Moon dasa bal 6y1m6d (computed in text).
- Saturn transit 1964 per star/sub portions (Mar64): KP sub-based transit. "Planet direct in beneficial sub gives gains; retrograde in same position gains assured but less remunerative for same pains."
- Transit judgement points (Mar64): lagna; house Moon owns; house transiting planet rules; house it transits; star it is in + houses of star lord; direct/retro; strength/eclipse; natal position; aspects. Guru-balam/gochara from Moon sign rejected (examples).
- Mars dosha (Jan64) – same as R4 (rejected). Dasa-sandhi, eka-dasa rejected (=R4). Poruthams (Apr64) traditional – rejected (=R4).
- Neecha-bhanga (Subba Rao) and medium longevity (Subba Rao) – traditional, NOT KP; ignore.
- Ruling planets of helpers: persons born with dasa lord as RP help; persons with 12th lord as RP harm (Moon period article). Not used for timing.
## MC_014 (Apr-May64)
- Planets friend/foe (Apr64): natural/temporal friendship "very insignificant". Decisive factors: (f) planet in 6/8/12 to house owned by it; (g) in 6/8/12 to dasa/bhukti lord; (h) aspect between planet and dasa/bhukti lord; (i) constellation and sub. => supports 6/8/12 rule.
- Moon in 4th by lagna & star (Apr64): results read via star lord's ownership from lagna; relatives' results by derived houses. Delineation, consistent with star-lord principle.
- Karaka-bhava-nasaya "in majority of cases does not come true" (again).
## MC_015-017 (May-Jul64)
- Day = sunrise to sunrise (confirmed again, May64).
- Lottery chart (May64): b. 23-11-1899 Libra lagna, sweep gain 8-5-1955 in Moon-Moon-Jup; money received 9-5-1955 10:30 AM (Mon, Jyeshta, Cancer lagna). Saturn dasa bal 5y2m4d. TEST CASE (time/place partial).
- NEHRU DEATH (Jun64 "Application of KP"): b. 14-11-1889 11:30 PM (corrected 11:27:30 LMT), Allahabad. Died 27-5-1964 between ~1:16-2:14 PM (actual ~1:44 PM). Rahu dasa Mer bhukti (from 12-11-1962) Rahu antara (27-1-1964 to 14-6-1964). Rahu in 12th (Gemini 12°49', own star Ardra, Mercury sub) = maraka; Mercury lord 3,12 = evil. Died on Wednesday (Mer), Jyeshta star (Mer), Virgo lagna (Mer) => RPs at death = bhukti lord. Saturn transiting Rahu's star Satabhisha in 8th from lagna. Sun transit in Mercury sub-sub (Taurus 12°46'...) with Rahu portion fixing hour. => death timing = dasa/bhukti/antara + RPs at the moment + Sun sub-sub. RULE: "Whenever Rahu is evil and causes death, the opposite node offers long life/good results".
- Earlier events same chart: wife Kamala died in Sun-Mer (Sun lord 2 = family; lord 8 to 7th; Mer lord 6 to 7th). Prison in Moon-Mer (Mer lord 12). Foreign honour in Mars-Mer (lord 3,12).
- Saturn in Satabhisha sub-by-sub good/evil: first 2° evil; 2°–3°47' not bad; 3°47'–5°54' evil (subs).
- Postal-strike mundane example: started on Moon in Rahu star (dasa lord Rahu), ended on Moon in Ketu star (Aswini) in 10th.
- Moon in 7 (Jul64): Moon in 7th in star of lord of 1,2,3,6,10,11 => success in election/competition/litigation; in star of lord of 4,5,7,8,9,12 => loss. (consistent w/ R6 6/11 vs 5/12)
- Divorce (Jul64): "planet in constellation of lords of 6, 8 or 12, connected with the 7th house and afflicted by Saturn, Mars etc. causes disharmony and divorce." Not Mars-dosha.
- KP finding (Jul64): the persons through whom gain/loss/friendship/partnership come during a dasa-bhukti have the dasa & bhukti lords as their ruling planets (birth day, star, sign, lagna). Marriage in Mars-X: spouse has Mars and X as RPs.
- Mars dasa: "all matters indicated by Mars come to pass in the bhukti of the lord of the constellation Mars occupies". Sub-lord decides nature of result. Journey: good while sub-sub of benefic runs; danger when evil planet's sub-sub operates.
- Benefactors: lord of 11, planets in 11 and in stars of occupant/owner of 11 (as RPs of the helpers).
- Profession by planets connected with 2/6/10 (e.g., tailor for police: Sun, Mer, Mars, Ven; shoe supplier Mars, Ven, Sat + 12th).
- Transfer to another department only when one enters/runs period & sub-period of planets indicating the new profession.
- Mercury in 2nd (Jul64): in star of / connected with lord of 2 or 6 => borrow; with 8 or 12 => repay loan / lend. Speculation: connected with lords of 1,2,3,6,10,11 (or in star of occupants/lords) => substantial gain; other lords => loss. Node in 2nd in Mercury sign gives Mercury's results. Reading method: lord of houses A,B in constellation of lord of C,D.
## MC_018 (Jul-Aug64)
- Kendradhipatya (Jul/Aug64): Jupiter as kendra lord affects only longevity, and only in the maraka dasa (Jupiter as bhukti/antara/sookshma). Survey: only 10–12% of Gemini/Virgo natives with Jup in 2 died in Jup dasa. "Jupiter causes unpleasant events only when in the constellation of evil planets, irrespective of house owned/occupied." => star-lord governs.
- Horary (number 1–108 = navamsa pada as Asc), Apr64:
  * Delivery: Moon (mind/query) lord of 5 in barren sign & Ketu star => already delivered. Date: Moon transiting Moon's sign on day ruled by Jup/Sat; birth on Sat Punarvasu = RPs (Jup, Sat, Moon). Lagna vargottama feminine => girl.
  * Profession from 10th cusp sign/star/sub at query: sign=department, sub decides profession.
  * TRANSFER: 10th cusp in common sign => transfer; fixed => stays; movable => leaves place & institution. Date = strongest significator transits the 12th cusp.
- Subba Rao rectification (prenatal epoch/adhana, Jul64) – traditional; NOT KP, ignore (KP uses RP method, R6).
- Moles by drekkana (traditional) – ignore.
## MC_019-020 (Sep-Oct64)
- Vimshottari dasa balance table and sub-division method (=R1/R3). Balance = remaining arc/800' × years. Sub-period = dasa years × planet years/120.
- Ashtama Sani & honour (Sep64): honour withdrawn in the period of planet that is 6/12 to dasa & bhukti lords (Rahu in 12 to Venus, 6 to Moon) – supports 6/8/12 negation rule.
- Wife's death example (Oct64): b. 21-9-1911 (Thu), Poorvapalguni; wife died when native 40, Rahu-Sat-Rahu. Rahu in 9th from the 7th conjoined with lord of 7th-from-7th (Saturn) => wife's death. DERIVED HOUSE method: "this is how every rule is to be applied" (count from relative's house).
- Mundane horary "food problem" 26-8-1964 8:30 PM New Delhi: India = Capricorn 12°20' (KP institute). Timing by Saturn's transit through star/sub; relief when Saturn turned direct 2-11-1964. Not for natal app.
- Strength of horoscope article (Subba Rao, shadbala, yogas) — traditional, NOT KP.
- Professions by Moon + combos (Moon in 10) – delineation list.
- Transit—correct method (Oct64, KSK): FINANCE/competition: houses 1,2,3,6,10,11 increase native's finances (self-acquisition, borrowing (6), service, business, friends); 4,5,7,8,9,12 = gains to opponent / drain / investment / lending / repaying / losing litigation-election. Borrowing timed in periods of 6th-house significators (occupants, planets in star of occupants, lord, planets in star of lord, conjoined). Twins differ by lagna (and aspects to lagna/Fortuna). Sookshma = antara × planet years/120.
## MC_021 (Oct-Nov64)
- "Limitation of the astrologer" (KSK speech 1-8-64):
  * Marriage chart F b. 16-7-1936 (Jup dasa bal 14y4m24d at birth): astrologer predicted marriage in Sat-Mer (Sat lord 7, Mer lord 2&11) – she got pleurisy (Sat also lord of 6, 7 = maraka); married only in Venus bhukti (Venus karaka, in 12th). LESSON: significators of 2/7/11 that also signify 6/8/12 etc. give those results; check ALL houses a planet signifies.
  * KSK's brother K.S. Gopalaswami b. 15-10-1916 6:12 PM, Kuthur (near Tanjore). Moon dasa bal 2y0m6d. Saturn dasa Mercury bhukti from 24-10-1962. Predicted "life in Bombay ends 28-6-64" (when Sun transits Mercury sub of Rahu star in Gemini, 12°33'–14°26'). Collapsed when Sun reached 12°33' Gemini on 27-6-64 afternoon; died 28-6-1964 4:45 AM, Moon in Saturn's sign (dasa lord). Sat-Mer-Rahu. Moon sign Taurus (fixed) => badhaka 9th; Rahu in 9 (Capricorn) = badhaka occupant; Mer lord 2 = maraka. => DEATH: dasa=badhaka/maraka lord, bhukti=maraka, antara=node in badhaka; Sun transit in sign of bhukti lord–star of antara lord–sub of bhukti lord. TEST CASE (death date known).
  * Same-day female b. 14-10-1916 9:15 PM (Moon dasa bal 9y11m29d), Taurus lagna & Moon; died Jan 1934 in Rahu-Rahu-Mer. Rahu in 9 (badhaka) in Saturn's sign; Mer lord 2.
  * Alternatives (change of residence vs death) to be resolved by checking spouse's and children's charts.
- LAGNA vs MOON SIGN (Nov64): if lagna weak (lagna occupied by 12th lord; lagna lord in 6/8/12 from lagna; malefics in 4 & 8 from lagna) and Moon sign strong, judge from Moon sign. **FLAG: early-period practice; later KP uses cusps from lagna only. Conflict to note.**
- Lord of 8 (Mars for Aries, Venus for Libra, Sun for Cap, Moon for Sag) harms longevity only if connected with maraka/badhaka lord or in their stars.
- KP HEALTH (Nov64, "Krishnamurti Paddhati"): health = lagna, its lord, occupants, planets in stars of occupants & lord, conjoined/aspecting. Disease = 6th occupants, planets in star/sub of occupants, 6th lord, planets in star/sub of 6th lord, connected planets. 8th = end of life unless connected with 11th. 12th = hospitalisation; 11th (12th to 12th) = discharge. Houses 1 & 11 = health/cure; 6,8,12 = disease. 12th to any bhava spoils it; 6th & 8th to any house detrimental to it.
  * DISEASE LOCATION: sign & house of the planet = part of body; star lord = health vs disease (houses it signifies); sub lord = system affected (e.g., Saturn sub = bones).
  * Planet gives results also in periods of planets in its star or sub.
  * Example: b. 23-6-1921 4:50 AM (place not given, Taurus lagna 23°), Moon Uttarashada, Sun dasa bal 1y5m3d. Leg-bone disease from Jup-Jup-Ven-Rahu (Mar 1959), drilling in Jup-Mer (11-2-64, 18-2-64), relapse Jup-Mer-Jup (May 1964). Venus lord 6 in 12th (Aries, legs) in own star Bharani, Saturn sub (21°53'20"–24°) => bones. TEST CASE.
- Badhaka 11th for movable lagnas: evil only for health/longevity, auspicious for other matters (Nov64).
## MC_022 (Nov64 "Readers ask")
- House to dwell in (Ganapathy, Nov64): 4th + Mars (buildings); Mars connection with 4th; lord 4 + lord 6 connected with Mars => house by borrowing/mortgage; lord 4 in 6 => mortgage; lord 4 in 8 / lord 8 in 4 => sell away. Example: house purchased in Sat-Jup-Mer (Mer lord 4) 8-6-1958 (chart incomplete).
- DELIVERY HORARY (17-9-1964 ~7:00 AM Thu, Delhi, number 33 → Cancer Aslesha 1st pada): daughter = 1st child → 5th; her delivery = 5th from 5th (9th). Child sex: lagna navamsa (Sag, masc.) + lagna lord Moon in masc. navamsa & in Sun's star => boy. Lord of 5 (Mars) in 12th bhava => hospital. RPs at query: day Jup (Thu), Moon sign Saturn (Capricorn), lagna lord Moon, lagna star lord Mercury. Event when day, star, sign and lagna all ruled by RPs: Monday, Poorvabhadra star, Aquarius Moon, Virgo lagna in Hasta. ACTUAL: boy born 21-9-1964 6:46 AM Mon, Delhi 28°40'N 77°12'E, Asc Virgo 14°02'. => TEST CASE for RP event timing.
  * Child order: 1st child 5th, 2nd 7th, 3rd 9th, 4th 11th (=R4/R6).
  * "Planet A delivers results according to nature of planet B in whose constellation A is."
- Election horary (Johnson 1964): candidate = 9th from lagna (stranger); success from 11th from his house: planets in star of occupants of 11 > occupants > planets in star of 11th lord > 11th lord (direct motion). Opponent's 11th lord in 12th from opponent & retrograde => opponent loses.
- OVERSEAS (natal + horary): b. 8-3-1928 5:27 AM, 8°11'N 77°29'E (query 20-9-1964 Delhi). Houses 3, 9, 12 => long journey/foreign residence; 12 + 6 => hospital; 12 + 8 => imprisonment. 3rd = 12th to 4th; movable 3rd => permanent change; common => goes and returns. Significators in order: (a) planets in star of occupants of 3/9/12, (b) occupants, (c) planets in star of lords, (d) lords, (e) connected. Mercury (plurality) => more than one tour. Timing: Jup dasa Sat bhukti Mars antara (1-3-1965 to 24-4-1965), Sun transit in Jup star–Sat sign–Mars sub or Sat star–Jup sign–Mars sub; departure on day/star/lagna ruled by Sat, Mars, Jup: predicted 13-3-1965 (Saturday, Punarvasu, Aries lagna before 9 AM). Result promised in Apr65 issue — CHECK.
- KEY: "planets which signify the result in horary will offer the result in the period & sub-period of those planets in the native's life (Vimshottari)".
- Leap year: Gregorian rule (1900 not leap).

Venus = karaka. Spouse's birth day, rasi and lagna ruled by the native's strongest marriage significators; marriage in conjoined periods of significators (or planets in their stars / conjoined); wedding day/star/rasi/lagna ruled by significators common to BOTH charts. "Natal RPs" (weekday lord, lagna lord, Moon sign lord, Moon star lord) = Jup, Ven, Sat: conjoined periods of natal RPs = advancement. ## MC_024 (Dec64-Jan65)
- Life reading (b. 7-4-1921) contd: injury 24-11-1951 motorcycle (Saturday). LONGEVITY: houses 1, 8, 3 = longevity; 12, 7, 2 (12th to each) = maraka. Fixed lagna → badhaka 9th. Strongest maraka significators: planets in star of maraka/badhaka lord (Rahu in Mars star) etc. Badhaka/yogakaraka Saturn gives good in its bhuktis (when not 6/8/12 to dasa lord) but kills in its sub-sub after longevity is exhausted. Predicted end in Mars-Rahu-Sat or Mars-Sat-Rahu (May 1992 / Oct 1994), confirmed by progression & transit (Sun/Moon in Mars star etc.). Health: periods of Jupiter/Mars good; Saturn (afflicting lagna) bhukti ill.
- Sade-sati rejected (Jan65): KSK honoured 20-9-1961 (Sat-Mer-Ven-Mars) and 20-12-1964 (Sat-Ven-Ven-Mars) during sade-sati. KSK chart: Capricorn lagna (Saturn in Pisces 3rd), Moon dasa bal 1y2m. Time of conferment worked out as lagna in Venus sub of Mars star in Saturn sign etc.
- LONGEVITY (Jan65): houses 1,8,3 longevity; 12,7,2 maraka. Badhaka: movable 11th, fixed 9th, common 7th. Strength of evil: planets in star of occupants of badhaka > occupants > planets in star of badhaka lord > badhaka lord. Kendradhipatya next (but "not always harmful"). Method: (a) dasa-bhukti-antara of joint significators, (b) progression, (c) transit, (d) horary chart at time of answering.
  * NODE RESULTS ORDER (KSK Jan65): node gives results of (1) planet conjoined, (2) planet aspecting it, (3) its star lord, (4) its sign lord — "this order is to be followed as this alone is correct". (cf. R6; node agency contradiction)
  * Example Cancer lagna (no birth data): hernia op 16-9-1964 (Wed, Moon in Poorvashada) in Mer-Mer-Ven; illness Rahu-Ven Nov 1924, Jup-Mer-Rahu 1935, Sat-Ven-Sat 1954; predicted ill-health Mer-Ven-Jup (Dec 1967), Mer-Sun-Rahu (Jun 1971), death not before 15-2-1973 (Mer-Mars-Sat, Sun in Aquarius Dhanishta Mercury sub).
  * Disease by star: evil planets in Visakha → hernia/appendicitis/diabetes; Poorvashada → diabetes/lungs (pancreas).
  * 7th = first wife; 2nd from 7th (8th) her maraka; second wife = 2nd house, her longevity 3rd (=8th from... uses 2nd from 2nd as maraka). 11th = intimate friend/live-in.
- FINANCE & FORTUNE (Jan65): b. Saturday 7-8-1920 7:20 AM LMT, 22°00'N 73°16'E. Lagna Leo (Saturn in lagna) vs Moon Aries (Ketu conj): resolve by RPs at birth/query (day lord etc.) — **early method, FLAG**. Houses 2 (bank), 6 (exertion/earning), 10 (profession); Sun karaka profession; Fortuna gives results of its sign lord.
  * Events: education ended Sun-Ket-Rahu (failed B.Com); joined bank end Dec 1941 Sun-Ven-Jup; to Rajkot 1-5-1942 Sun-Ven-Ket; joined International Bank 6-5-1942; left bank → Govt Revenue in Moon-Mars-Sun; May 1962 Rahu-Jup-Sat; father died 27-6-1964 (Sat, Uttarashada, Moon in Saturn sign) in Rahu-Sat (Saturn = badhaka lord for 9th house Aries [movable → 11th from Aries = Aquarius]). Predicted: overseas 28-6-1966 (Rahu-Mer), promotion Nov 1965 (Rahu-Sat-Ven), Oct 1968, retirement ~21-3-1980 (Jup-Sat-Ket). TEST CASE.

  * RETIREMENT: houses 1, 5, 9 (12th to 2, 6, 10) and 3, 5 (6th/8th to 10th...) end service; Sun transit in sign/star/sub of dasa-bhukti-antara lords.

Evil planet in 4th + period of 12th lord => thorough change of residence. Occupant stronger than owner; node in sign of X & conjoined with X stronger than X.

Dasa lord Jupiter in Rahu's star AND Rahu's sub; Rahu conj Mars (lord 8) → Jupiter gives 8th-lord results; bhukti lord Venus (lord 2 & 9 in 11) in Saturn's star (lord 6) & Rahu sub → "Jup-Ven = like 8th-lord dasa & 6th-lord bhukti". Event day 3-4-1965: Saturday (star lord of bhukti lord), Moon transiting conj Rahu. RULE: "judge a planet by the lord of its constellation and sub". Kendradhipatya of Jupiter "applies to health alone".
- ENGINEER: b. 11-2-1924 (time not given), Venus dasa bal 10y5m?; married 3-8-1941 in Moon-Mars (wife: Cancer lagna, Libra rasi, Chitra = Moon, Venus, Mars); appointed 7-8-1946 Moon-Mer; resigned 1-6-1965 Rahu-Sat (Saturn lord 2 exalted in 11 — "new cycle of life").
- MARRIAGE PREDICTIONS (Aug65):
  * Srikakulam bride (Moon dasa bal 5y0m28d; Jupiter lagna lord): "Cusp to cusp alone should be taken as a house" (Sun in 2nd sign but in 1st bhava). Significators Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Mercury, Venus; nodes in their signs (Ketu with Mercury & Venus in Jupiter sign; Rahu in Gemini with Saturn) represent them. Predicted fixation 23-2-1966 (Sun in Satabhisha, Saturn sub; Sun reaching Moon's degree at query) and MARRIAGE 19-4-1966 in Rahu-Sat-Sat-Mars. → CHECK.
  * M.V.R.S. (Visakhapatnam) daughter, Ketu dasa bal 6y1m20d: significators Sun, Moon, Ketu, Venus; Venus aspected by Saturn delays till Saturn bhukti over; predicted fixation end-Aug 1965, MARRIAGE ~5-11-1965 in Ven-Ket-Sun; groom's RPs Venus/Ketu/Sun (Krittika or Aswini trine). Venus in 4 → groom in same town; lord 7 in 5 → not related. → CHECK.
  * H.M.R. (MP): married in Sat-Rahu though neither "connected" sthoola-wise; Rahu in Aquarius represents Saturn, aspected by Jupiter & Sun in 11 → strongest significator.
- DEBTS (KP, Aug65): M. Rao b. 10:00 PM 9-3-1923 (Hanamkonda?), Libra lagna, Mer dasa bal 7y11m26d. BORROW in dasa/bhukti of: (a) planets in the constellation OR SUB of occupants of 6th, (b) occupants of 6, (c) planets in constellation or sub of 6th lord, (d) 6th lord, (e) conjoined/aspected. REPAY in periods of: (a) planets in star/sub of occupants of 12 or of a benefic in 8, (b) occupants of 12 & benefic in 8, (c) planets in star/sub of 12th lord or of 8th lord if natural benefic, (d) 12th lord & benefic 8th lord, (e) conjoined/aspected. Benefic in 8 (= 2nd from 7th) → creditor/borrower lucky → repayment. Sun (lord 11) in Jupiter's star (lord 6) & sub of lord 12 → borrows & repays repeatedly. Predicted debt-free 20-11-1966 to 4-1-1967 (Moon-Rahu…). NOTE: "star OR SUB" — first explicit sub-lord use in significator lists (1965).
- Udu dasa results per Sarvartha Chintamani/Parasara (exaltation, house position etc.) — traditional; ignore.
- Combustion limits quoted from Jataka Parijata (Aug65 Udu dasa article, traditional): Moon 12°, Mars 17°, Mercury 14° (retro 12°), Jupiter 11°, Venus 10° (retro 8°), Saturn 15°. → third combustion spec (FLAG with R3 8°30' and 5/10/15).

## MC_037 (Oct 1965)
- Sarvartha Chintamani / Parasara Moon-dasa & bhukti results by exaltation/house/6-8-12 to dasa lord (traditional; FLAG — not KP method; only usable rule echoed: bhukti lord 6/8/12 to dasa lord = adverse; lords of 2/7 = maraka illness).
- CASE marriage + father's death same moment: b. 19-9-1942 7:03 AM, 24N49 86E42 (Venus bal 9y2m21d). Married 13-2-1960 Moon-Rahu-Mer (Saturday, Moon in Ketu star; Ketu agent of Saturn since in Saturn's sign & aspected by Saturn -> node "represents" Saturn for weekday). Father died same period Moon-Rahu-Mer.
  * Uses MOON SIGN as lagna because lagna afflicted (Mars L8, Sun L12 in it) and Moon sign aspected by Jupiter — FLAG (early-period practice).
  * Node rule: if node in a significator's sign, node is stronger than that lord; take planets in the node's stars.
  * Editor: "Ninth house represents father" (5th from 5th logic: native is 5th from father's 9th... i.e., father = 9th). Father's longevity: count houses from 9th (father's lagna). Badhaka for father from that sign (fixed -> 9th from it).
  * Longevity significator order (KSK): (a) planets in star of occupants of badhaka; (b) occupants of badhaka; (c) planets in star of badhaka lord; (d) badhaka lord; (e) planets in star of kendradhipati and maraka lords; (f) if none strong, 6, 8, 12. Maraka houses 2, 7, 12 (12th to 1, 8, 3). Node conjoined with kendra lord acts as kendradhipati.
- Anjaneyulu "general status": lagna lord, Moon's star lord, navamsa lagna lord, dispositors — strength (traditional, contributor; FLAG). Case 9-10-1904.
- Pars Fortuna (KSK defends): PF = Asc + Moon - Sun. Lottery/race case b. 23-?-1899 (Saturn bal 5y2m4d), won 8-5-1955 Sunday Moon-Moon-Sat antara (4-5 to 21-6-1955), day = Moon transiting star of PF's sub lord (Saturn). Fortuna in Mer sign, Jup star, Sat sub. Planets in star & sub of PF sub lord give the fortune. KSK also invokes Western orb conjunctions across sign boundaries (Sat & Rahu within 2° in different signs = conjunct) — note: KSK says conjunction by degrees not sign. Western aspects to judge favourable — FLAG early.
- KSK rejects Ashtakavarga (fails for twins).
- Nadi: KSK's test; most nadis not genuine (no rule).
- Marriage case bride b. 19-2-1938 (Rahu bal 13y11m4d); Jup-Rahu bhukti 29-8-1965 to 23-1-1968. "Though Rahu in 2nd sign, not in 2nd bhava" -> bhava = cusp based. Owner weaker than tenant, esp. node. (prediction continues next issue)

## MC_040 (Nov/Dec 1965)

Gochara (Saturn in 3 good / 8 evil for whole 2.5 yrs) rejected — cannot time. Within a dasa, each bhukti gives different results (marriage in one, illness in Rahu bhukti, bereavement in Saturn...).
- KEY RULE restated (KSK, KP book preview): "The lord of the constellation shows the nature of the result, i.e., the houses OWNED by the star lord manifest. The favourable/unfavourable SUB denotes success or not. When? Conjoined period." Lord of 1 -> gains by own effort; 3 -> through brother/publication; 4 -> mother etc. Sub eliminates among star-lord's possible significations.
- Sravana vs Sathabhisha (illustrative).
- Teacher's profession (contributor Athalye, SAYANA Western aspects) — Mercury+Jupiter with 1, 2, 5, 10. FLAG (non-KP contributor).

Bhava starts AT the cusp (KSK: ascendant is beginning of house, not midpoint) — confirmed statement. Interview time = when ascendant governed by significators. Sai Baba b. 23-11-1926 sunrise 6:26 AM IST Puttaparthi 14°22'N 76°58'E (Arudra; Rahu bal 1y2m5d); predictions Mer-Rahu 25-7-1972 to 13-2-1973 peak; Ketu dasa 28-1-1980 to 28-1-1987 silence (CHECK, unverifiable).
- LUCKY STARS (KSK, IMPORTANT for scoring): Houses 1, 2, 3, 6, 10, 11 = victory, success in competition/elections/games/betting/litigation, financial benefit; 7, 8, 9, 12, 4, 5 = favourable to opponent. House-pair grading for a planet owning two houses (its stars/sub occupied or transited):
    VERY GOOD: 1&6, 2&7, 2&11, 6&7, 6&11, 10&11
    GOOD: 1&2, 1&10, 2&3, 2&9, 3&6, 3&10, 6&9, 7&10, 9&10
    MIXED: 2&5, 4&11, 5&6, 5&10, 8&11, 11&12
    BAD: 1&4, 3&4, 3&8, 3&12, 4&7, 4&9, 7&8, 8&9, 9&12
    VERY BAD: 1&8, 1&12, 4&5, 5&12, 5&8, 7&12
  Sun/Moon own one house: their stars good if they own 1/2/3/6/10/11. Planets in star OR SUB of such a planet give the result in their periods. Example Aries: Mars (1,8) self-destructive; Venus (2,7) good (= 1,8 to opponent; ex-parte decrees); Mercury (3,6) success after enmity; Moon (4) good for 4th matters but loss in competition; Sun (5) children but speculation loss; Jupiter (9,12) no success; Saturn (10,11) certain success.
  (NOTE: pair 2&7 "very good" though 7 is opponent — because to opponent it's 8&1.)
- Bala: 12th bhava (traditional/Western). 12 = 4th from 9th = father's property after death (traditional). "Not occupation/lordship of 12th matters most but the constellation in which..." (KSK note).
- PROFESSION (KSK, b. 19-3-1937 6:04 PM Madras surgeon; other reader b. Dec 1944): UNIVERSAL PRINCIPLE "whenever matters of a house are judged: (a) lords of the SIGN, STAR and SUB of the house (cusp); (b) planets in star of occupants; (c) occupants; (d) planets in star of lords; (e) lords; (f) conjoined/aspected." => FIRST explicit mention of cusp sign/star/sub lords (Jan 1966). Include all planets within the bhava extent (cusp to cusp). Node represents its sign lord ONLY when neither conjoined with nor aspected by a planet. Doubt resolved by RPs at time of answering (29-11-1965 Mon, Sravana, Saturn sign...). Profession indications by planet combos (Sun = Govt, medicine; Mars surgery/engineering; Mercury diagnosis/sales; Moon+Saturn oil...). Predicted career start Sat-Moon-Rahu April 1967 (CHECK).
- CORRECT AYANAMSA (S. Kannan): Spica - 180°, Newcomb 50.2486"/yr; 1-1-1962 = 23°18'54" (Lahiri 23°19'23"); 1-1-1965 = 23°21'25". ## MC_044 (Mar/Apr 1966)
- KSK (Readers ask, Gemini Jupiter in 11 in Venus star): KEY: "the result indicated by the bhava/house occupied is WEAK, whereas the matters signified by the lord of the constellation are enjoyed; whether one gains or loses in such matter is decided by the SUB." Even exalted planet gives evil if in star of lord of 8/12 (Aquarius-born Venus exalted in 2nd suffers in its period if in star of L12 Saturn/L8 Mercury, esp. if sub also detrimental). For Gemini: Jupiter (7,10) own star -> gain via partner/litigation; in Mars (6,11) star or sub -> profit as opponent loses; Venus (5,12) star -> pleasant expenses/loss in competition; Sun sub -> short journey for investment; Saturn sub -> heavy loss (possibly away from native place); Mercury sub -> purchase property at high price; Ketu sub -> judge Ketu.
  * GENERAL PRINCIPLE (5 steps): (1) lagna; (2) houses owned by each planet; (3) stars ruled by each planet; (4) matters of the STAR LORD's owned houses enjoyed; (5) success/failure, profit/loss, pleasure/pain, reunion/separation by the SUB.
  * SEPARATION via sub: planets A,B in star of L7: A in sub of L2/L11 or occupants of 2/11 -> marriage/meeting/reunion; B in sub of L12 or occupant of 12 -> separation in B's period. Cause by L12's other ownership: with 5 or 11 -> delivery; with 7 -> disharmony; with 3 -> short journey; with 9 -> long journey/higher studies overseas/stay away.

## MC_050 (Jun 1966)
- MARRIAGE—HOW LONG TO WAIT (KSK, girl b. 16-2-1946 6:43 AM, Aquarius lagna 7°, Ketu bal 5y11m12d): RULE stated: "first note whether lagna unafflicted; if afflicted, consider whether Moon sign more auspicious; IF BOTH AFFLICTED TAKE LAGNA." Saturn retrograde + aspecting Moon delays till its period over; then planets not aspected by Saturn give marriage. RPs at answer (Wed, Swathi, Libra Moon) = Mercury, Rahu, Venus -> Ven-Mer-Ven-Rahu Oct 1968; "another school": Sun transits Moon's degree at query (Libra 7°) = 20 Oct; Jupiter in 11th from that Moon (Leo) = 1968 -> marriage 20-10-1968. CHECK.
- Gnana Pradeepika ch.6 lost articles (traditional, FLAG).
- HOW TO JUDGE WHETHER A PLANET IS BENEFIC OR MALEFIC (KSK, KEY ARTICLE):
  * Benefic/malefic is relative to the matter & relative: same planet may promote native and kill wife.
  * House-wise benefic test (traditional-ish): (A) health: lagna lord unafflicted, not in 6/8/12, not with malefics; (B) lord of 2 benefic for 2nd matters if not 6/8/12 from 2nd and not conjoined with lords of 6/8/12 from lagna; similar per house.
  * "PLANET is the SOURCE; CONSTELLATION (star lord) indicates NATURE of result (houses owned by the star lord); SUB is the DECIDING factor whether favourable or not." Example Scorpio: planet in Punarvasu (Jupiter L2&5) + Mercury sub (L8&11) gives child; same star Venus sub (L7&12) no child.
  * Planet A gives its results mainly through planets in its 3 stars; A itself gives predominantly matters of its star lord. Planet's NATURE shows how (Jupiter fair means, Saturn underhand, Mars foul); its LORDSHIP shows through whom (L1 own effort, L3 brother/publishing, L4 land/vehicle/mother, L5 speculation/children); WHAT happens (gain/loss, birth/death, marriage/widowhood) is shown ONLY by the lord of the constellation.
  * Cases: Pisces-born, Jupiter (L1&10) in P.Bhadra (own star) Aquarius 12th bhava, sub of lord of 11&12 (Saturn) -> promotion in Jupiter dasa. Leo-born, Saturn in Pushya in 12th (Cancer): marriage gains through partner (Saturn L7). Sagittarius-born, Sun & Moon in Anuradha (Saturn star, L2&3) in 12th bhava: Moon Venus sub (L6&11) gain; Sun Mars sub (L5&12) speculation loss in Sun dasa. Gemini-born Sun in Mrigasira (Mars L6&11) in 12th, Jupiter sub (L7&10): promoted, posted to jail (Sun-Mars-Rahu transfer to Jail Dept; Sun-Jup-Mars selected/promoted). 12th-bhava placement did NOT give loss -> house occupied is weak; star lord rules.
  * DASA READING ALGORITHM: for dasa lord note (a)(b) houses ruled by its star lord; (c) these matters = mainly what the dasa promises; (d) favourable or not decided by its SUB lord (note only houses owned by the sub lord — "do not scrutinize further"). Event fully experienced in A dasa, B bhukti (B = A's star lord), C antara (C = A's sub lord).
  * Do the same for every planet (what each can offer per its star & sub).
  * Dasa-bhukti relationship (FLAG, Western): bhukti lord at 18°, 30°, 36°, 54°, 60°, 72°, 108°, 120°, 126°, 144° from dasa lord co-operates; at 22.5°, 45°, 67.5°, 90°, 112.5°, 135°, 150°, 157.5°, 180° does not.
  * Dasa continuity: results of dasa A continue in dasa B only if A and B connected (sign, star, sub or Hindu aspect); else B dasa brings thorough change in B's star-lord bhukti and sub-lord antara.
  * Changes in dasa A occur in sub-periods of planets 6/8/12 to A and those in adverse (Western) aspect to A. FLAG.
  * Malefic = planet in star of lord of 6/8/12 (relative to matter). In malefic dasa, bhukti lords in good aspect co-operate with the mischief; in adverse aspect obstruct it (native enjoys). FLAG (aspect-based).
- MANY ASPECTS TO A HOUSE (KSK): house = cusp to next cusp (Western/Placidus) — "the editor follows". "Always the CUSP of a house is the strongest and indicates only the matters of that house." Aspects (Western, transit & progression) to cusp vs to planets in house: cusp aspect governs house matters; planet aspect governs that planet's lordship matters/relatives. Progressed Sun-Venus conjunction must aspect 7th/11th cusp favourably for marriage. FLAG (Western aspects/progressions — early period).
- TIMING OF MINOR EVENTS (KSK 4-4-1966 Delhi ~5:30 PM): RPs = Moon (Monday), Sun (Uttaraphalguni), Mercury (Moon in Virgo), Mercury (Virgo lagna). Find zodiac point ruled by these as sign/star/sub: Mercury sign, Moon star (Hasta), Sun sub = Virgo 20°40'-23°20' -> rises ~6:24 PM; friend arrived exactly 6:24 PM. CONFIRMED. For major events use Vimshottari periods jointly ruled by the 4 RPs (day lord, Moon star lord, Moon sign lord, lagna lord) "at the time the question is ANSWERED (when astrologer has urge to answer), not when put". Birth-Moon fixing by horary number (curiosity).
- VIPARITA RAJA YOGA (KSK): Uttarakalamrita rule (lords of 6/8/12 in 6/8/12 or mutual exchange, unconnected with others = rajayoga). KP version: lord of 8 in star of lord of 6 and lord of 6 in star of lord of 8 produces the yoga (opponent loses: 6 = 12th from 7, 8 = 2nd from 7); malefics owning 6/8 stronger in this than benefics. Example: friend won election in conjoined period of L6/L8 exchange. Not possible for Capricorn by sign exchange (Mercury/Sun). (Traditional with KP reinterpretation.)

- *** HOW TO ASCERTAIN WHAT A DASA LORD SIGNIFIES (KSK, core rules) ***
  1. Planet D (in whichever bhava) offers matters of the bhava OCCUPIED BY THE LORD OF ITS CONSTELLATION (predominantly; the star lord's occupation).
  2. D offers results of the house it occupies to a good extent IF NO PLANET is in D's constellation. If any planet is in D's star, D gives its own occupied-house results only to a negligible extent; the planet in D's star gives the results of the house occupied by D to a good extent.
  3. Sources (how/through whom) = D's nature and the house D occupies (relatives/matters of that house).
  4. Houses OWNED by D flourish or perish depending on D's position from its own signs: if D is in 6/8/12 to one of its signs, that sign's results suffer; the other prospers (Jupiter exalted in Cancer: Pisces matters favourable, Sagittarius destroyed — Cancer is 8th from Sag).
  5. D gives results of another planet P if conjoined with or aspected by P.
  6. Strength depends on (A) strength of star lord and (B) D's own situation: star lord exalted/own sign -> D strong even if D is debilitated; D exalted but in star of a debilitated planet -> little; D debilitated & weak in star of a planet neither exalted nor in own sign -> cannot give to desirable extent; D exalted in star of another planet P (P not debilitated) -> very good. Same for own sign/friend/enemy.
  7. SUB: if the sub lord of D is a significator of houses 1-2-3-6-10-11 -> desirable; if sub lord signifies 4-5-7-8-9-12, then even though star lord arranges desirable results, in the sub-sub period of the sub lord there will be failure, disappointment, denial, delay. [NB: this 1,2,3,6,10,11 vs 4,5,7,8,9,12 split is generic for "material gain" matters.]

KP: "single method; for each aspect refer to certain houses; significators by a single rule; SUB decides the fate of the matter." "KSK may fail; KP applied systematically never fails." "RPs at the moment of judgment is the panacea."
- PENNILESS—GAJAKESARI (KSK): b. 29-7-1931 19°18'N 84°51'E (Berhampur), asc 26° (Taurus?); Moon dasa bal 9y3m; Rahu dasa prince; Jupiter dasa from 29-10-1965. Transport business from Nov 1965 with partner born Taurus lagna, Pisces rasi; lost all (lakhs) June 1966-June 1967. Jupiter 9°49' Cancer = Moon sign, Pushya (Saturn) — Saturn in 2nd, owns 3 & 4 (restricting) -> Jupiter gives 2nd house (finance). Whether finance improves or deteriorates = SUB lord by occupation, ownership, nature: Jupiter in Venus sub; Venus in 8th house (9th sign), L7 & L12 -> loss, spend, waste through Venus matters (music, ladies, vehicles, jewels) — actually transport. Person responsible: born on Venus day or Venus sign lagna/rasi — partner was Taurus lagna. CONFIRMED.
  * RULE restated: "A dasa lord offers matters which the lord of the constellation by occupation, ownership and nature denotes"; improvement/deterioration by sub lord's occupation, ownership, nature.
- HINTS TO CAST A CHART WITH KRISHNAMURTI EPHEMERIS: KSK ephemeris gives sidereal time for 5:30 PM LMT at 82°30'E and planets for 5:30 PM IST. Steps: find nearest town lat/long (1° ≈ 71 miles); ghatis/vikatis after sunrise -> hours (1 ghati = 24 min, 1 vikati = 24 s, 2.5 vikati = 1 min); LMT = sunrise + interval; IST = LMT ∓ 4 min per degree from 82°30' (east of 82°30' subtract, west add). ST at birth = ST at previous 5:30 PM + interval + 10 s/hour correction; longitude correction negligible (±10 s). Planets: interpolate between 5:30 PM IST before and after birth (proportion (60X+Y)/1440; add if direct, subtract if retrograde). Hindu day starts at sunrise. [Implementation note: equivalent to standard ephemeris interpolation; Swiss Ephemeris will do this directly.]
- *** NO MORE DOUBTS (KSK, Bombay 9-7-67) — CUSPAL SUB LORD THEORY ***
  * Promise of an event (will he marry at all? go overseas at all?) cannot be told from significators alone (every chart has 2/7/11, 3/9/12). Twins / births within 15-50 min share sign, star, planet bhava positions and aspects; only the cusp moves -> only the SUB of the cusp changes -> "sub and sub alone".
  * MARRIAGE: note the sub lord of the 7TH CUSP. If it is one of the significators (of marriage), marriage may be looked for; if that sub lord is DIRECT, marriage will surely take place; if RETROGRADE, many chances arise and nothing materialises. Do not take 2nd & 11th cusps for legal marriage (2 = relatives, 11 = friends/keeps).
  * OVERSEAS: sub lord of the 12TH cusp: if it is a significator of 3, 9 or 12 AND is in the constellation of the occupant or owner of a WATERY sign -> goes overseas. If also it occupies or owns a DUAL sign -> more than once.
  * MULTIPLE MARRIAGE: if the sub lord of the 7th cusp is in the constellation of the occupant or owner of a dual sign -> marries more than once.
  * Sub lord DIRECT -> attempts succeed; RETROGRADE -> falls through.
  * CHILDREN: 5th cusp sub lord must be a significator of 2, 5, 11. SPECULATION: 5th cusp sub lord also a significator of 6 & 11.
  * General: primary cusp and secondary houses: marriage 7 primary, 2 & 11 secondary; change of residence 3 primary, 12 secondary; education 4 primary, 9 secondary; accidents 8 primary, 12 secondary; children 5 primary, 2 & 11 secondary; profession 10 primary, 6 & 2 secondary. The primary cusp's sub lord must be a significator of the matter's houses.
  * CUSP RECTIFICATION BY RPs: take the moment of judgment; RPs = lords of lagna, star, rasi, day. If several cusps are asked, take the FIRST one mentioned (asc; or 7th; or 8th; or 11th...). The cusp's sign lord and star lord must be in the RP list; one of the remaining RPs is the sub lord, with PRIORITY: lagna lord first, then nakshatra (star) lord, then rasi lord, then day lord. If day lord and rasi lord coincide with the person's lagna & star lords, the sub lord = lord of lagna and sub-sub = nakshatra lord at query. "Invariably comes correct."
  * Prenatal epoch useless (two births in same hospital); Western experiment with medical expert found it never correct.
  * Two persons asking together: e.g., Thursday 1-6-67, Cancer lagna, P.Bhadra star, Moon in Aquarius; both born Capricorn: RPs Jup (day & star), Saturn (rasi), Moon (lagna) -> Saturn & Moon = lagna Capricorn Sravana (Moon star 10°-23°20'); sub Jupiter for both. Two Aries-born 10 min apart, query Tuesday, Visakha, Libra rasi, Gemini lagna -> RPs Mars, Jup, Ven, Merc: Aries (Mars); Venus star Bharani 13°20'-26°40'; first-born Jupiter sub Merc sub-sub; second-born Merc sub. Mercury-ruled people come with others (plurality).

- *** WHAT IS KRISHNAMURTI PADHDHATI (KSK summary statement) ***:
  * Requirements: place, time (IST/LMT/ghatis), Raphael's Table of Houses and Raphael's Ephemeris for the year.
  * A house commences at one cusp and extends to the next; house longitudes unequal (equal only when ST ≈ 6h and latitude 14°-18°N).
  * "The SIGN indicates the strength of a planet posited therein." Lord of a house = owner of the sign on which the cusp falls.
  * House results are given in the periods of: (1) planets in the constellation of the occupants; if none, (2) the occupant itself; if house unoccupied, (3) planets in the constellation of the house lord; if none, (4) the lord itself; also (5) planets conjoined with or aspected by significators.
  * "The NATURE of the result depends on the lord of the constellation; the SOURCE on the planet by its occupation and ownership. The SIGN indicates the strength, the PLANET the source, the STAR LORD the nature of the result, and the SUB LORD whether the matter will fructify or not, how it will proceed, when it will culminate, and whether the ultimate result is favourable."
- Thiruvenkatacharya critique: marriage given as Sat-Merc-Moon vs Kalyanam's Sat-Sat; KSK: conjunction across sign boundary (29° Leo & 0° Virgo) is still conjunction — "no wall in the heavens" (so KSK accepts cross-sign conjunctions by degree proximity).
- Ojha's article (Astrological Magazine Aug 1967) claiming nakshatra-dispositor theory is old; students reply: no ancient literature on planets in stars and none on 9 sub-divisions; KSK's sub is original.
  * Students' summary of KP (endorsed by KSK): (1) sign = strength; (2) planet = source; (3) nature of result = star lord by its occupation & ownership of bhavas; result fructifies in the period/sub-period of the planet posited in its constellation — "a planet offers results indicated by the lord of the constellation occupied by it, and NOT what it has to give according to the bhava it occupies"; (4) the SUB lord decides favourable or not, and a sub lord may be beneficial for some matters and detrimental for others. Traditional tara rules (2/4/6/8/9 stars good, 3/5/7 bad; janma/anujanma/trijanma) and "fourth dasa evil" rules rejected.
  * TRANSIT STAGES THROUGH SUBS: if the star lord signifies marriage, when a significator transits that constellation (with favourable dasa/bhukti running), matters move: in one sub negotiation starts; in the next (unfavourable) sub the attempt fails; in a favourable sub marriage is fixed; in a sub of an unconnected planet days pass; when again in sub of a planet promising marriage, celebration takes place. Dasa+bhukti cover ~32 months; sub transit pinpoints. Same for law suits (hearings, judgment, outcome).

- *** GOCHARA—TRANSIT (KSK) ***
  * A transiting planet A reaching the natal position of B "kindles" B's matters through A's source; "it is the POINT in the zodiac which decides the nature of the result; the transiting planet shows how it is caused, its source".
  * SUB DIVISION = constellation divided in proportion of Vimshottari years, starting with the star's own lord then cyclic (company/shares analogy: 800 units × years/120). Sub arcs (constant): Sun 0°40', Moon 1°06'40", Mars & Ketu 0°46'40", Jupiter 1°46'40", Mercury 1°53'20", Rahu 2°00', Saturn 2°06'40", Venus 2°13'20". Examples: Rohini starts 10° Taurus: Moon sub 10°-11°06'40", Mars 46'40", Rahu 2°...; Ardra from 6°40' Gemini: Rahu 6°40'-8°40', Jupiter 8°40'-10°26'40", Saturn 10°26'40"-12°33'20"; Ashlesha 16°40' Cancer: Merc to 18°33'20", Ketu to 19°20', Venus to 21°33'20", Sun to 22°13'20". Equivalent to dasa-bhukti division of Moon's star at birth. "My own discovery."
  * SIGN = strength; STAR lord = nature; SUB = whether favourable to the native and to whom (a significator of 8 & 12 helps the opponent/other party). Note planets evil to native; transits through their stars produce those results.
  * PROCEDURE: (1) cast with Raphael ephemeris & tables; (2) cusps; (3) bhava of each planet; (4) planets in stars of occupants; (5) planet A in star of occupant B causes results of house occupied by B in A's dasa/bhukti/antara and when a planet transits A's star; (6) subs ruled by those planets give results desirable or not — success/failure, profit/loss decided by sub alone; (7) classify good/evil planets; favourable constellation + beneficial sub = fruitful. Pipeline/tap and sweets-basket analogies.
  * Each sub's quality = sign lord + star lord + sub lord read together (Bharani: Mars sign movable; P.Phalguni: Sun fixed; P.Ashadha: Jupiter common).
  * MARRIAGE STAGES EXAMPLE: Saturn dasa; significators Saturn, Sun, Mars. Mars transiting U.Ashadha (Sun star, Saturn sign): Rahu sub — no talk; Jupiter sub — purchases; Saturn sub — girl interviewed; Mercury sub — negotiation; Ketu sub — fear of falling through; Venus sub — they see each other; through Sravana; Dhanishta: Mars sub — mediator; Rahu sub — no progress; Jupiter sub — betrothal date fixed; Saturn sub — betrothal; Mercury sub (Aquarius) — invitations; Ketu sub — prayers/temple; Venus sub — relatives, music, feast; Sun sub (with Sat dasa Mars bhukti Sun antara) — marriage.
  * Rule: "When a planet transits a constellation, the nature of the result signified by the house in which the lord of the constellation is tenanted will come to light." Planet A gives results of its star lord's bhava; also its own bhava results provided no planet in its constellation; also results of sign(s) it owns provided no planet in those signs and none in its constellations. (example chart follows)
  * Gochara (cont.): example — Sun in 9 in Moon's star (Moon in 11) gives 11th results (if Moon were in 4, 4th); Leo vacant with 12th cusp in Leo -> Sun = L12 also gives 12th; Moon(2) in U.Ashadha (Sun star) gives 12th matters. If no planet in Sun's stars, Sun gives 9th (occupation) & 12th (ownership). If 10th star lord, planets transiting its constellation bring 10th matters "irrespective of the name of the planet". Each bhava: occupant's three stars; if vacant, the three stars of the lord of the sign where the cusp falls — transits there bring that cusp's matters.
  * "The CUSP denotes the genuine results of that bhava." Planets in a sign but behind the cusp belong to the previous house. An evil transit (e.g. Saturn) over a cusp gives undesirable results for that house; affliction to a planet in a bhava does not afflict the bhava itself — only the other matters the planet signifies suffer. A cusp receiving good aspect protects the house's matters; good Jupiter on a cusp protects it. (FLAG: aspects to cusps.)
- ECLIPSE / COMBUSTION (traditional summary): orbs Sun 17°, Moon 12°, others 7°; combust within 5° of Sun (real), 10° ordinary, none beyond 15°; Moon conjunction = samagama (good); various authors (Jataka Tatva, Sanketa Nidhi, Uttarakalamrita: Venus & Saturn don't lose strength; Horasara). KSK conclusion (end of article): an eclipsed (combust) planet "does not become a malefic and deny the results it has to offer, but does so to a lesser extent" (example: Dhanus lagna with combust Mercury had marriage and professional prosperity in Mercury dasa). => Combustion: minor weakening only; no denial (FLAG for scoring: optional small weight).
- KP vs Thiruvenkatacharya (KSK): Surya Prakash (Raman's son) b. 5:40 AM IST 2-11-1935 Bangalore (12°58'N 77°38'E): KSK verifies 5:40 AM using KP (ST 8h01m49s; lagna 29°50' Libra sayana?; 2nd 29°19' Sco; 3rd 28°19' Sag; 4th 28°19' Cap; 5th 29°19' Aqu; 6th 0°19' Ari); critiques "rectification" to 4:51 AM LMT.
