## MC_000 (Apr–May 1963) [detailed redo]
- Vol 1 No 1 April 1963 contents: Saidapet astrologer (P.S. Raman); Foreword (V. Thiagarajan); What next (planned topics); Astrology an occult science; History; Use and limitations; Karma and destiny; Branches (medical, meteorological, judicial, natal, horary, electional, kabala, kerala, omens). [Much text = Readers duplicates, removed.]
- Bio: KSK (Tanjore; retired after 34 yrs at King Institute, Guindy; founded Modern Astrological Research Institute (MARI), Madras & Delhi; devotee of Uchishta Maha Ganapathi); avoids "likely/probably" in predictions. Claimed predictions: trouble in the North (1962 China war), share price fall after 15-10-1962 (18 months in advance). No dated chart data.
- PLANNED TOPICS (60): includes Western aspects (orbs, applying/separating, translation of light, refranation), Vimshottari/Ashtottari/Kalachakra dasas, progression, Western & Hindu transit, ashtakavarga, shadbala, all life matters (longevity, health, finance, siblings, education, vehicles, property/mother, speculation, servants/debts, marriage/divorce, partner/litigation, will/legacy, father/religion/higher education/competitive exam/overseas, profession/honour, friends, treachery/imprisonment/expenses/hospital), horary, market forecasting, mundane, medical, stellar astrology; "51. Why stellar astrology is to be followed AND Western aspects are to be applied in all cases"; rectification & pre-natal epoch; Thirukanitha vs Vakya panchanga; which ayanamsa is correct. => In 1963 KSK still intended to use Western aspects together with stellar astrology (EARLY-PERIOD; later KP downplays aspects except node/significator connections; FLAG).
- KARMA: no chance or accident; events are products of causes (karma). Catalogue of predicted events (luck, longevity, accident, marriage, inability to go against destiny) — details removed as Reader duplicates. Phone diagnosis of a patient's unconsciousness from a distance. "Astrologers should not alarm the public with terrors; convey serious events with sympathetic concern, mildly, but should not omit to mention it." (Relevant to the user's "be direct" requirement: KSK says mention serious events, but mildly.)
- USE & LIMITATIONS (1963 view): judicial astrology "correct though incomplete".
  * Western Sun-sign transit and Hindu Moon-star (gochara) systems cannot explain opposite results for people with same Sun sign / same birth star (election rivals; litigants and judge all born in Bharani; two Rohini girls marry in different years; exam pass/fail lists cover all stars). Gochara by star alone incomplete — a complete chart is needed.
  * 1963 PROMISE (pre-KP): "cent per cent success" by taking positions of ALL planets incl. Uranus & Neptune and Fortuna at birth vs all transiting planets "along with the several aspects being formed", "with a modification to be published". FLAG: early Western-aspect method; superseded by KP star/sub.
  * ASCENDANT more important than Moon: race example — ascendant's aspect decides success, Moon's aspect decides mental satisfaction; both good = wins & elated; both bad = fails & mopes. "Take the ascendant primarily, then Moon, Sun and other planets, their lordships and occupations." (FLAG aspects.)
  * TWINS 24 min apart, same lagna sign & planet signs: one selected at interview, other not — explained (1963) by exact degrees and Western aspects of planets to the Ascendant (later KSK explains by cuspal sub — consistent idea: exact cusp matters).
  * FORTUNA "deserves serious attention for transitory results" (FLAG Western).
  * Popular transit predictions: by date of birth (Sun sign) or by rasi/nakshatra (Moon); if only nakshatra known, find its pada for the sign; if both, read both signs (~8% born near New Moon have both same). Examples (25 July Swati -> Libra & Cancer; 15 April Bharani -> Aries; 3 Dec Anuradha -> Scorpio; 10 April Sravana -> Pisces & Capricorn). "Transit results from all planets & ascendant at birth vs all transiting planets alone will be accurate; these (sign-based) are general." — OUT OF SCOPE.
- Vol 1 No 2 May 1963 contents: What follows; Horary astrology; Shanti to ward off evil; Learn and earn; Seven and its sanctity; The Earth; The Heavens; Different kinds of time.
  * Branches: Electional (choosing times), Kabala (numerology, later), Kerala (prediction from face / flower name), Omens (nimitta) — traditional; REJECT for engine.
  * SHANTI (1963): remedies likened to vaccination — not a guarantee but reduce virulence, give mental strength, "more often save the individual" (cholera example: 4 friends). [CONTRADICTION with later KSK (1967): fate cannot be changed by homa/japa/muhurta; later only colour remedies "some shanti" if Jupiter/L9 aspects DBA lords.] FLAG; remedies out of scope.

## MC_001 (May–June 1963) [detailed redo]
- "Seven and its sanctity" (KSK as 'Kayeskay'): seven ages of man mapped to planets (Sun = father/conception, Leo = 5th of zodiac = progeny; Moon = infancy/mother (Cancer); Mercury = schooling, speech, writing — afflicted Mercury = lying/thieving; Venus = lover/matrimony — spoiled Venus = unbearable married life; Mars = warrior/courage/independence; Jupiter = wise judge, wealth, reputation, over-weight; Saturn = old age, longevity, emaciation, teeth falling, chronic disease, depression, cataract, prostate). Sign rulership order mirrors planetary distances. [Karaka descriptions — usable as natural significations (karakas); no timing rule.]
- The Earth / The Heavens: poles, equator, latitude/longitude, International Date Line; celestial equator, ecliptic (obliquity 23°27'), equinoxes, right ascension, declination, celestial latitude (planets within ~8° of ecliptic), parallels. Astronomy — implementation via ephemeris.
- DIFFERENT KINDS OF TIME: sidereal day ~4 min shorter; mean vs apparent solar time (equation of time; eccentric orbit, obliquity); civil day from midnight; IST meridian 82°30'E chosen 1906 (+5h30m). LMT = GMT + 4 min per degree E. Examples: 12:15 PM IST Madras (80°15'E) = 12:06 PM LMT; Calcutta (88°24') +23m36s -> 12:38:36 LMT; New York (74°01'W, EST −5h; US zones fixed 18-11-1883) 3 PM EST = 3:03:56 PM LMT.
- SIDEREAL TIME at birth = ST at previous Greenwich noon − (2/3 s × E longitude°) [or + for W] + interval since local noon (LMT) + 10 s/hour interval correction. Worked: Agra 78°05'E 6:30 PM IST 15-5-1963 = 6:12:20 PM LMT (ST at Greenwich noon 3h29m59s); Allahabad 81°54'E 3:30 AM IST = 3:27:36 AM LMT; Ahmedabad 72°38'E 12:05 PM IST = 11:25:32 AM LMT -> ST 26h54m40s (−24h). Boston, San Francisco examples. "Never start working without the place of birth."
- VOL 1 No 3 JUNE 1963 contents: Ephemeris; Sidereal time; Signs & houses; Dates of standard-time adoption; Sun—Who is who; Ashtama and Athrishta.
- EPHEMERIS: Raphael's Astronomical Ephemeris "best and most popular"; columns explained (ST at Greenwich noon, Sun longitude/declination, Moon longitude/latitude/declination, planets, Moon's node = Rahu (MEAN node as printed by Raphael); Ketu 180° away).
- SIGNS & HOUSES: zodiac 12 × 30° from vernal equinox (here sayana description); rising times vary with latitude (Aries ~80 min, Gemini 118 min at 34°N; equator Gemini 129 min); ~1° per 4 min. House systems: Hindus follow Sripati paddhati; Western countries ~8 methods (continues). [Implementation: KSK later mandates Placidus.]

## MC_002 (June–July 1963) [detailed redo]
- HOUSE SYSTEMS listed (equal-quadrant variants, Morinus, Campanus (prime vertical), Placidus semi-arc "in common use", Regiomontanus modus rationalis). KSK: use RAPHAEL'S TABLE OF HOUSES ALONE ("best; others vary widely"); sayana cusps for 10, 11, 12, Asc, 2, 3 per latitude 2°-60°N by ST. Signs always 30° from (moving) vernal equinox; houses vary in length. => ENGINE: Placidus (Raphael-equivalent).
- DATES STANDARD TIME ADOPTED (table): USA & Canada 18-11-1883 noon; Japan 12-7-1886; Austria/Hungary 7-9-1889; Germany 1-9-1890 (Prussia 1-4-1893); Romania/Serbia/Bulgaria 1-10-1891; Italy 1-11-1893; Denmark 1-1-1894; Switzerland 1-6-1894; Norway, Queensland, Australia 1-1-1895; NSW, Victoria, S. Australia 1-2-1895; Philippines 11-5-1899; Alaska 20-8-1900; Spain 1-1-1901; Atlantic time/New Brunswick/Nova Scotia 15-6-1902; South Africa etc. 1-3-1903; INDIA 1-1-1906; Peru 28-7-1908; Chile 1-1-1910; France 10-3-1911. GMT offsets table (India +5:30; Ireland 0:25:21; Singapore 6:55:25 etc. — historical). => ENGINE: use IANA tz database (handles pre-standard LMT, Indian war time).
- SUN delineation (traditional): mythology, names; planetary cabinet (Sun king, Moon queen, Jupiter & Venus ministers, Mercury prince, Mars commander, Saturn servant). Monthly prediction samples (out of scope).
- VOL 1 JULY 1963 contents: What follows (planned topics repeated, incl. "Western aspects applied in all cases"); Latitudes & longitudes of places; 12 signs & influence; Sun in houses; Moon; Can we dodge fate.
- PLANETS IN ASCENDANT — physical features (traditional/Western, karaka-level): Jupiter = overweight (esp. in its periods), Saturn = emaciated (thin in Saturn period), Venus = pleasing countenance, Moon = medium height, pale, plump, Mercury = tall, prominent features, active, Saturn = lazy, Mars = muscular, Sun = dignified, Uranus = long limbs, eccentric, Neptune = some defect; beneficial Saturn rising = much hair. (FLAG: descriptive; not timing.)
- ARIES delineation applies (1963) to Aries ascendant OR Moon in Aswini/Bharani/Krittika-1 OR Sun-sign dates 14 Apr–13 May (Nirayana). Sayana/Nirayana explanation: vernal equinox now ~7° Nirayana Pisces (~23° precession; ~50"/yr; Sun at sayana 0° Aries 21 March; nirayana 14 April). "Hindus follow Nirayana and I personally advocate the stellar theory."
  * Character, marriage compatibility (Aries women suit Leo/Sag husbands; Libra calms) — traditional sun-sign; FLAG/out of scope.
  * PROFESSION combos in Aries (traditional): strong Mars in 10th as L2/L6/L10 = defence; Sun+Mars = army medical/surgeon; Saturn in Aries = butcher; Saturn+Venus = barber/saloon; weak Jupiter = crime police; +Venus = traffic police; Saturn+Mercury = CID; Saturn+Mars+afflicted Mercury = thief; Sun+Mars = goldsmith; Jup+Mars+Venus = jewellery; Venus+Mars = tailor; Sun+Venus = boxers/sports goods; Mercury = mechanical engineers. (Karaka combinations; later KSK uses significators of 2/6/10 + karakas.)
  * PLANETS IN ARIES in lagna — Sun (exalted), Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn (debilitated; unhappy married life — partner jealous or dies), Rahu (= Mars results), Uranus, Neptune descriptions with aspects (FLAG). Moon afflicted by Saturn = danger of drowning; by Venus = trouble through women; afflicted luminaries = changes of occupation.
- SUN IN HOUSES (1963, Western-aspect based, FLAG): Sun's results modified by sign, star ("which lunar mansion") and house — "absolutely essential" to include the star occupied. House of Sun by birth time (first 2 hrs after sunrise = 1st; 2-4 hrs = 12th; noon = 10th cusp; 6-8 PM = 7th; 4-6 AM = 2nd). Sun in 1: in beneficial Sun 6-yr dasa — security of service, govt help, honour; afflicted by Mars = short temper, sunstroke, fever; Saturn = low recuperation; Uranus = undiagnosable fever; Jupiter = losses; Venus = wife's sickness, domestic disappointment; Moon = vacillating. Sun in 2: 2nd = right eye (male), left eye (female); spectacles in Sun sub-period if afflicted; wealth through father, govt job; Mercury+Sun orator. *** 1963 SECOND-MARRIAGE note: 2nd house = 8th from 7th = longevity of first wife; Venus & Sun in 2nd in a common sign in evil aspect (22.5°) => second marriage (after first wife's death or divorce). [Consistent with KSK later: 2nd = second marriage; dual signs = plurality.] Sun in 3: upachaya (3, 6, 10, 11 improving houses) ... (continues).

## MC_003 (July–Aug 1963) [detailed redo]
- SUN IN HOUSES (cont., Western-aspect based; FLAG): 3rd = younger siblings (born in Sun sub-sub period if well aspected), neck/arms/ears, editing, short journeys, own religious duty; Sun afflicted by Mars & Saturn = partition of property among siblings. 4th: Hindus = mother, Westerners = father; MARI stellar research supports Hindu (4 = mother). HOUSE MEANINGS: 3rd = INCLINATION to a subject; 4th = actual study / regular schooling; 9th = higher studies. 4th = land, house, permanent property (2nd = movable); Mars = bhoomi karaka; Venus = vahana karaka; Moon aspect to Sun in 4 = mother's health. 5th: mantra initiation (fruit judged from 10th); pleasures, speculation, children; Sun in 5 = few children, delivery difficulties; barren sign unaspected by Jupiter = no child; 5th = 11th from 7th = gain through wife's luck / business partner (Uttarakalamrita); 5th = advisers/ministers. 6th: upachaya; 6th = 12th from 7th = opponent's loss -> native wins competitions; disease (movable sign: nervous, bilious; fixed: asthma, heart; common: liver, pulmonary, chronic); servants; labour; medical service; pets; 6th = 3rd from 4th = MATERNAL UNCLE; overdraft/bank help needs Jupiter; enemy relative by afflicting planet's nature/lordship.
- MOON delineation (traditional): diseases, political/economic significations, places (Brihat Samhita), numbers (2, 9 fortune; 8 unfortunate), colour white, gems (pearl), deities by aspecting planet (FLAG). PROFESSION: consider sign & CONSTELLATION of Moon and aspects; "the lord of the DASA which operates is more important": example — Saturn dasa: kerosene/petrol firm (Saturn + Moon = liquid from earth); Mercury dasa: textiles (Moon + Mercury). => profession changes with dasa lord's significations (consistent with later KP).
- CAN WE DODGE FATE (KSK Rotary Club Delhi 23-5-1963): karma & reincarnation; seeds-and-rain analogy; "genuine nadis" can tell past births. 1963 VIEW: remedies of ancient Maharishis "remove the evil and offer desired results" (to be continued). [CONTRADICTION with later KSK (1967): fate unalterable, remedies cannot change destiny.] FLAG.
- VOL 1 AUGUST 1963 contents: Erection of horoscopes; Table of ayanamsa; Taurus; Mars; Setting Sun (Sun in 7th); Moles; Letters; No more doubts; Position of planets; Can we dodge fate.
- ERECTION OF HOROSCOPE (worked): Agra 27°10'N 78°05'E, 6:30 PM IST 15-5-1963 = 6:12:20 PM LMT, ST 9h42m29s; interpolate Raphael's Table of Houses for Agra between ST 9h41m23s and 9h45m16s: sayana 10th Leo 23°17', 11th Virgo 25°17', 12th Libra 24°17', Asc Scorpio 18°48', 2nd Sag 18°17', 3rd Cap 20°17'; opposite cusps +180°. "For Nirayana, Dr. Raman's and Mr. Lahiri's [ayanamsa] may be used" (1963!) [CONTRADICTION: from 1964-ish KSK insists only KP ayanamsa; "other ayanamsa -> prediction fails"]. TEST: cusp calculation check (Placidus) for engine validation.
- TAURUS: sayana 21 Apr–21 May; nirayana ~14 May–14 June (Krittika 2-4, Rohini, Mrigasira 1-2; ~3d8h per pada). Earthy, fixed, feminine, semi-fruitful, short ascension (traditional descriptions). Saturn is a benefic for Taurus (owns 9 & 10) -> rise to the top. Profession: Venus, Saturn & Mars = footwear (12th = feet). Countries/cities. Afflictions -> petty thefts, forgery (Saturn & Mercury). Pray Lakshmi (FLAG).
- MARS delineation (traditional): Mars-Moon/Mercury aspects (FLAG); body parts (external sex organs, muscles, left ear, head, bladder, nose, prostate, rectum, marrow); products; Tuesday; months June-July.
- SUN IN 7TH (Western): *** DERIVED HOUSE MEANINGS of 7th (useful) ***: marriage, love affairs, partner's description, law suits, SECOND CHILD (3rd from 5th), business partners, younger brother's child (5th from 3rd), MATERNAL GRANDMOTHER (4th from 4th), father's elder brother or father's friend (11th from 9th), friend's father (9th from 11th), death of secret enemy (8th from 12th), maraka to native. 1st = longevity; 7th = short life; 8th = longevity, 7th = 12th from 8th = loss of life. 7th also = doctors attending, bank lending/holding deposits, engineer building house — anyone one transacts with.
  * Sun in 7: good aspects -> gain from marriage; malefics in 7 -> marital displeasure (Mars, Saturn); Sun in 7 = barren woman when in barren sign & star of malefic/effeminate planet (Ardra, Hasta fruitless; Mercury & Saturn effeminate); Sun in 7 at sunset births -> prefer compromise in litigation; afflicted -> litigation drags and lost. (FLAG traditional/Western.)
- MOLES: number/shape/colour meanings (traditional) — REJECT/out of scope.

## MC_004 (Aug–Sept 1963) [detailed redo]
- MOLES by body part (ankle, arm, armpit, back, belly, breasts, buttocks, cheek, chin, ear) — traditional; REJECT.
- Letters: N.N. Krishna Rau (most Indian horoscopes from vernacular panchangas don't check with standard ephemerides like Raphael/Lahiri/Raman; bhava chakra rarely drawn); B.V. Raman (29-6-1963) welcomes the magazine.
- *** NO MORE DOUBTS Q1 (KSK, 1963) — earliest star-lord rule ***: two friends b. Sunday 16-9-1928 6:15 AM (Virgo lagna, Sun & Moon in Virgo) and Sunday 24-8-1930 7:05 AM (Leo lagna, Sun & Moon in lagna; Moon in Makha); place Bombay (18°55'N 72°54'E, per editor's note). First gains on nakshatra-birthdays (Moon returns: Moon = L11 for Virgo), second loses (Moon = L12 for Leo; Moon in Ketu's star Makha -> worry when Moon transits Makha). On calendar birthdays (Sun return) results reverse (Sun = L12 for Virgo, L1 for Leo). RULES: (1) a transiting planet returning to its natal position gives results of its LORDSHIP; (2) "when we judge results by transit, consider the constellation the planet was in at birth, its lord and his lordship: planets in the constellation of a malefic (by lordship) give undesirable results; in the constellation of benefics (by lordship) agreeable ones"; (3) TORCH & SLIDE: planet = torch; star lord = coloured slide; "mostly the results of the lord of the constellation will be enjoyed". [Consistent with later KP.]
- Q2 KENDRADHIPATYA: (details mostly Reader duplicate) — example native with IAS first attempt, married at 26 in Mercury sub-period to bride born in Revati (Mercury star, Jupiter sign); child born in Visakha (Jupiter star, Venus sign) in Venus sub-period, coinciding with promotion; another promotion in Mars sub-period => SPOUSE'S/CHILD'S BIRTH STAR LORDS relate to the native's period lords (early form of "relatives' RPs = native's DBA lords"). Conclusion: natural benefics owning kendras are evil ONLY for health & longevity when they own 4 & 7 (not for other 4th/7th matters).
- CAN WE DODGE FATE (cont.): 1963 karma classification by chart (FLAG — traditional/speculative): (a) DHRUDA (unpardonable) karma: lords of 6, 8, 12 (or Rahu/Ketu) in lagna and Jupiter neither in lagna, nor with Moon, nor aspecting lagna or Moon -> must suffer; remedies give negligible relief (~1 in 25,000); (b) DHRUDA-ADHRUDA (pardonable): no malefics in lagna, or evil planet in lagna aspected by Jupiter -> remedies seem to work; (c) ADHRUDA: Jupiter in lagna or with Moon, aspecting evil planets/lords of 6/8/12 -> enjoy without remedies. Examples of destiny: twins after separating from wife; baby falling into a basket; wife killed in derailment while husband survived; Madras physician predicted to recover in May 1958 and die 26-6-1958 — CONFIRMED (no birth data); Parikshit; Caesar; Johann Stöffler. Remedies list (talisman, gems, prayer, charity, feeding, fasting, pilgrimages, holy baths, yoga, muhurta, lucky days/colours/numbers). Astrologer advises remedy just before a good period starts (cynical/explanatory). => Remedies OUT OF SCOPE; destiny view = events predetermined.
- VOL 1 SEPTEMBER 1963 contents: Gems; Talisman; Moles; Sun in houses; Rising Moon; No more doubts; Table of ayanamsa; Erection of horoscopes; Permanent calendar.
- GEMS (traditional, Jataka Parijata): Sun ruby, Moon pearl, Mars coral, Mercury emerald, Jupiter topaz (pushparaga), Venus diamond, Saturn blue sapphire, Rahu gomedh, Ketu cat's eye. Gem karaka Venus; stars Mrigasira & Revati; sign Aquarius. Acquisition of gems in periods of planets so connected; planets in 6/8/12 to them -> dispose. Bride gets diamond in Venus sub-period, red stones at Seemantham in Mars sub-period. Colour preference by benefic/malefic planets (girl Virgo lagna, Hasta: Mars L8 -> red unlucky; Mercury & Venus green/white lucky). KSK (1963): choose gem/colour by the DASA & BHUKTI lords running. REJECT (remedies; out of scope).

## MC_005 (Sept 1963) [detailed redo]
- GEMS (cont.): "The ascendant is the most vital point" (Kala Vidhana: day > tithi; nakshatra > day; lagna strongest). "In all cases, unless the ascendant is much afflicted and the Moon sign is comparatively stronger and more favourable, the ASCENDANT alone must be taken." Planets in ascendant modify the sign's results; consider lagna lord, its sign, aspects. Gem of a planet forming favourable aspect; never the gem of the lord of 6/8/12 or of planets in their constellations (enemy-recommends-nephew parable). Gem meanings (ruby, diamond, emerald, pearl, coral, yellow/blue sapphire, agate, amethyst, beryl, topaz, cat's eye, gomedh) and sign birthstones. REMEDIES — out of scope.
- TALISMAN (1963): effective via tapobala of the maker; acquiring an effective talisman coincides with the start of one's good period. REJECT/out of scope.
- MOLES (cont.) — REJECT.
- *** SUN IN 8TH HOUSE — 8TH HOUSE MEANINGS ***: longevity, kind of death, worries, property of the deceased, wills & legacies, partner's share of profit, DOWRY & income through wife (2nd from 7th), sickness of brothers (6th from 3rd) and of his enemies?, mother's gains (5th from 4th? — "mother's gains with least effort"), father's loss/stay outside/hospitalisation (12th from 9th), unearned wealth. Sun born 2-4 PM is in 8th. Sun in 8 (unless own sign) weakens constitution; critical period mid-life esp. in Sun dasa; if the native's FINAL dasa is Sun's -> heroic/valiant death. Sun in 8 = widowhood/widowerhood in Sun's period/sub-period unless mitigated; Jupiter's good aspect -> survived by partner (FLAG). Legacy from superior; gain from relatives born on Sunday, Leo lagna, or Sun stars. Dowry if Sun owns 6 or 11 and marriage in Sun sub-period. Service in medical/chemical/insurance.
- *** SUN IN 9TH — BHAVA vs SIGN (1963) ***: "Sun in the 9th house" = 9th in the BHAVA chakra (cusp-based), not by sign; results MODIFIED BY THE LORD OF THE SIGN in which Sun is (e.g., Aries asc 24°, Sun early Capricorn = 9th bhava but Saturn modifies, not Jupiter who owns the 9th cusp sign). [Early form of KP bhava logic.] 9TH HOUSE MEANINGS: father (Hindu), merit of previous birth, providential help at the last moment, bhagya, higher studies, long journeys, voyages, foreigners/strangers (not necessarily abroad), temples, religion, philosophy, dharma. Sun in 9: danger to father = father's end coincides with native's Sun period/sub-period (after father lives his own span) [rule: relative's death timing via native's periods]; protection in adverse periods; professional studies; with good aspect from L12 -> overseas, foreign contracts; Sun ruling 2/7/11 in 9 -> bride from a family NOT already related; younger brother's litigation/marriage (7th from 3rd); mother's illness/loan (6th from 4th); children's speculation gains (5th from 5th); wife's younger brother (3rd from 7th); elder brother's ambition (11th from 11th); afflicted by Sat/Mars/Venus -> secret affair with guru's partner (FLAG).
- SUN IN 10TH — 10TH HOUSE MEANINGS: honour, reputation, authority, worldly standing, profession, permanency & promotion, business, patrimony, employers, king/orders issued; younger brother's legacy/longevity (8th from 3rd), mother's business partner or end of life (7th from 4th), ill-health to children (6th from 5th), wife's permanent possession (4th from 7th), father's self-acquisition (2nd from 9th), elder brother's loss/hospital (12th from 11th). Sun in 10 (10 AM-noon; digbala): promotion/authority in joint periods of Sun and a favourably aspecting planet; reversion with afflicting planet; made permanent. Sun as matrimonial indicator (connected with L2/L7/L11) in 10 -> bride from same place/street/co-tenant; love marriage against parents in Saturn-Sun period. Sun L12 in 10 -> loss by theft (stolen article judged from 10th in horary).
  * *** PROMOTION (1963) ***: needs 10th AND 6th (service) and their lords well aspected; if 10th good but 6th afflicted -> instead the MAID-SERVANT delivers a child (10th = 5th from 6th; 6th = servant). Both good = increase in income & status. (Answers the famous "maid-servant" question.) => Derived-house logic: same period can manifest through a relative/servant's house.
  * Sun in 10, native's Sun periods: brother's health anxiety/insurance-legacy gains; mother's death or business partner/litigation; children's sickness/borrowing; wife buys land; father's acquisitions; elder brother's loss.
- RISING MOON (Moon in ascendant): Moon rises ~52 min later each day; Moon in lagna in each sign (Aries ... Aquarius) — character delineations (traditional; FLAG). Moon in Leo: sincerity false if 4th has a malefic or lagna lord evil conjoined with evil. Moon in Sagittarius (6th from its own sign): death possibly due to overwork/haste. (descriptive only)

## MC_006 (Sept 1963) [detailed redo]
- **Moon in Aquarius/Pisces in lagna** (reader answer): "for more correct results note in which constellation Moon was" — star lord governs over sign. Confirms the stellar principle early.
- **"No more doubts" — sade-sati (KSK 1963)**:
  - Sade-sati is NOT always harmful. Judge Moon's exact longitude, which houses Moon and Saturn rule, and which relative Moon indicates.
  - Tamil proverb (1st round moderate, 2nd good, 3rd bad) = meaningless (REJECT).
  - Sade-sati spans intermittent periods due to Saturn's retrogression.
  - Correct reading: take the Moon sign as 1st and judge Saturn's lordships from it. E.g. Aries rasi: Saturn = L10 & L11 → profession/gains, not misery.
  - A planet owning two houses gives BOTH results, they don't cancel. Pisces rasi: Saturn L11 (gain, friend's aid) and L12 (loss, repayment of loan) — both happen.
  - Quote: "Those who predict using the Stellar method can give the nature of events correctly, and including Western aspects the exact date." FLAG: 1963 Western-aspect timing view; later KSK times by RPs/sub transit.
  - FLAG overall: sade-sati/gochara from Moon sign is traditional; KSK here reinterprets rather than endorses. Not to be used in app (no rule beyond "Saturn transit gives results of houses it signifies").
- **Erection of horoscope** worked example: Allahabad, 3:30 AM IST, 15-5-1963, sidereal time 18h55m18s. (Method: LMT correction + ST from ephemeris + Raphael's Tables of Houses — useful for validating chart-casting code.)
- **Longevity critique (KSK / editor)**:
  - Traditional reading refuted: Saturn L8 from Chandra lagna aspected by Mars = short life; Moon in 8 with weak Jupiter = alpayu. REJECT (yoga-style).
  - The girl actually died in Mercury dasa – Venus bhukti – Mercury antara, NOT in Jupiter or Saturn–Mars as predicted traditionally.
  - Badhaka: movable lagna → 11th; fixed → 9th; common → 7th. Marakas 2 & 7; for a common lagna the 7th is both badhaka and maraka.
  - FLAG (early practice): lagna weak (Ketu in lagna, lagna lord in 7th from it) and Moon in own sign → read from the Moon sign (Cancer). From Cancer: Venus = L11 = badhaka lord, in Saturn's star (Saturn L8 from Cancer); Mercury = L12, in Saturn's star = maraka/badhaka connection → death in Mercury–Venus–Mercury. CONFIRMED (death occurred). Later KSK uses lagna/cusps only — contradiction noted.
  - Principle: death period = joint period of planets signifying badhaka/maraka, deposited in stars of 8th-connected planets.
- **Rasi palan Sept 1963 (K. Ganapathy)**: Moon-sign transit forecasts — out of scope (gochara, REJECT).

## MC_007 (Sept–Nov 1963) [detailed redo]
- **Rasi palan (Aquarius, Pisces) Sept 1963**: Moon-sign transit forecasts — out of scope (gochara, REJECT). Note it reads derived houses for relatives (brothers = 3rd, wife = 7th, father = 9th, mother = 4th, children = 5th) from the Moon sign — derived-house idea is consistent with KP, the transit method is not.
- **Gemini sign delineation** (sun-sign style): Sayana Sun in Gemini 22 May–23 June; Nirayana 15 June–16 July. Character, professions (book-keepers, accountants, clerks, agents, editors, journalists, teachers, etc.), marriage compatibility by sign (Gemini matches Libra, Aquarius, Aries, Leo). FLAG: sun-sign delineation — not usable for timing. One rule-like line: with Mars/Saturn/Venus ruling 6/8/12 contributing, secret love affairs, loss; "a few may marry twice" (dual sign → plurality — consistent with later KSK rule that dual sign = more than one).
- **"Can we dodge fate" — gems (Punarvasu, contd.)**: REJECT (remedies). Content: Western month birth-stones criticised (calendar month ≠ Sun sign); Sepharial and Varahamihira planet/sign colours; rule offered: use colour/gem of the strongest of lords of 1, 9, 10 that is well placed and unafflicted; avoid colours of the dasa lord when it owns/occupies/is connected with 6/8/12. Example: Gemini lagna, Saturn L8 in Cancer in own star Pushya, aspected by Jupiter from Pisces → use Jupiter's yellow, not Saturn's blue. Out of scope for app. Rule fragment worth keeping as a statement of early KSK: "a dasa will be evil when the dasa lord owns or occupies 6/8/12 or is connected with them."
- **No more doubts Q1 — dasa year length**: Soura Manam (solar year, Sun from Nirayana Mesha 0° to Mesha 0°, ~365.25 days) is to be used for Vimshottari (Udu dasa); NOT 360-day Savana or ~354-day Chandra Manam. **APP RULE: dasa year = sidereal solar year (~365.2564 days); KSK elsewhere uses 365.25 — check Readers note.**
- **No more doubts Q2 — planet owning two houses**: (mostly duplicate of Readers.) Example: Virgo lagna father, Chitra 1, Moon in lagna, Saturn in Aries in 8th, running Saturn dasa — Saturn L5 & L6 from Virgo, in 8 — read both lordships plus occupation (details in Readers).
- **No more doubts Q3 — yogas operate only in the periods of yoga-forming planets**: analogy of judge on duty. Example Aries lagna, Moon+Mars in Aquarius (Chandra-mangala yoga) — only in their dasa/bhukti. Lord Rama: Gaja Kesari (Moon + exalted Jupiter in Cancer) — would have operated only in Jupiter dasa; he suffered in Saturn (L8 to Cancer) dasa (left with Viswamitra) and Mercury dasa (exile, father's death, separation from wife, war); ruled only in Venus dasa (Venus exalted in 9th). FLAG (yoga discussion, but the principle "results come only in the period of the planet or of planets in its star" is core KP).
- **No more doubts Q4–5 — longevity critique (continuation of MC_006 item)**:
  - **Case A (girl, Sagittarius lagna, Ketu in lagna, Moon in Cancer (own), Jupiter L1 in 8, Venus L6 in 8)**: author's alpayu yogas (all benefics in 8, Saturn L8 from Chandra lagna aspected by Mars, lords of 1 & 8 weak) all REJECTED. Birth balance Jupiter 2y 2m 29d. Test of KSK: if Jupiter caused short life she'd die in Jupiter dasa; if Saturn+Mars, in Saturn–Mars bhukti (age 15). Actual death: **Mercury dasa – Venus bhukti – Mercury antara** (CONFIRMED). KSK principle: "results indicated by a planet will be given in full by that planet in its period, or by planets in its constellations."
    - Badhaka: movable lagna → L11; fixed → L9; common → L7. 8th = longevity, 12th from 8th (= 7th) = loss of life → 7th is maraka for all; for common lagna (Sagittarius) 7th is both maraka and badhaka.
    - From lagna: Venus L6 in 8 = disease, difficulty, death.
    - FLAG (early practice — Moon sign as lagna): lagna weak (Ketu there, lagna lord in 8th) and Moon in own sign → count from Cancer. Then Venus = L11 = badhaka lord (movable sign), in star of Saturn (L8 from Cancer); Mercury in Saturn's star and owns 12th (Moksha) → Mercury maraka + badhaka. "Generally for Dhanur lagna, Mercury will prove to be a maraka."
  - **Case B (born 18-1-1915, died 8-10-1938)**: author said death in Jupiter–Saturn; KSK recomputes: balance Mars 0y 10m 23d → Rahu dasa 18y → Jupiter dasa starts ~11-12-1933 (KSK table: Jup-Jup to 18-1-2…, Saturn bhukti ends 11-8-1938) → so 8-10-1938 is **Jupiter dasa – Mercury bhukti**. Mercury = L7 (maraka & badhaka for common lagna) and occupies the other maraka house 2, and is in 12th from the dasa lord ("planet in 12 from the dasa lord, which gives health/longevity, causes danger to life"). Lagna is a common sign (details of chart not fully legible). CONFIRMED-ish (date given). **Rule: planet in the 12th from the dasa lord can cause death in its bhukti.** (Check against later KSK – not repeated later as far as noted.)
  - **Case C (female, born 13-4-1909, died 5-12-1934)**: author's reason (lagna hemmed by malefics, no Jupiter aspect; Ketu in 12) REJECTED. KSK: Mercury L7, Saturn L2 & strongest planet in the 2nd, Mars, or planets in their stars cause death. Rahu in 6th (disease) aspected by Saturn (maraka lord) and in Mars's star; nodes give results of planets they're connected with more than those planets, and memorable events. Saturn (maraka lord) conjoined with Mercury (badhaka lord) and in Mercury's star → evil. Died **Rahu dasa – Saturn bhukti** (CONFIRMED), not Rahu–Ketu.
    - Principle: "Saturn, chief governor of longevity, generally gives death in its period if it has any connection with the death-inflicting planet."
    - Principle: "any interpretation should be applicable to all cases born under similar conditions."
- **Sun, your friend (11th house)**: Sun in 11 good if not lord of 6/8/12. 11th = friendship, hopes, wishes, reunion, fulfilment, children (5th from 7th), long journey/higher education of younger brother (9th from 3rd), difficulties to mother (8th from 4th), marriage of children (7th from 5th), speculative gains to spouse (5th from 7th), short journeys of father (3rd from 9th), health/success of elder brother. Derived-house listing useful for app (relatives). Sun in 11: long life, wards off evil; Sun-period: gains via friends/government; Sun+Mercury in 11: maternal uncle support, business, PR career.
  - Relatives during Sun-in-11 period (derived houses): brother success in exam / overseas; mother — difficulties, may give ornaments/cash (11 = 8th from 4th = paying to another); children — marriage/partnership; maternal uncle (6th) — success in competition/election, but 6th from 6th → ill health/borrowing; wife — gain via speculation/music/cinema, childbirth; father — transfer (3rd from 9th); elder brother — health trouble (eyes, heart, BP esp. Sun in Pisces). 12th from 12th = opposite of 12th's indications ("easy gains").
  - Aspects (Western: Jupiter, Venus, Saturn good/adverse to Sun; semi-square 22.5° bad): FLAG Western aspects; Hindu aspect variant: Mars from 4/5/8 → police/military, Jupiter from 3/5/7 → law/religion/bank. FLAG.
- **Nov 1963 issue contents**: Mercury, Sun (contd), Moon the wanderer, Mars, overcoming fate, prayers (REJECT remedies), Sun secret foe, Moon unsteady fortune, Mars independent, No more doubts (King or Kubera; eclipsed planet), transit table. Most pages duplicate Readers.

## MC_008 (Nov 1963) [detailed redo]
- **Sun in 11 (contd.)**: parable of birds/hunter/vulture — 11th = 12th from 12th, destroys secret enemies (12th). Native with Sun in 11 protected from anonymous petitions, false reports; witnesses unknowingly exonerate him. Derived-house principle: **12th from a house negates that house** (12th from 12th → defeat of secret enemies). Descriptive; no timing rule.
- **Moon, the wanderer (Moon in Ascendant)** — mostly Western-aspect delineation, FLAG throughout:
  - Moon's sidereal cycle 27d 7h 43m. Moon = mother (night birth), personality, womb.
  - Planet near the ascendant cusp modifies features/character; judge blend of sign, lagna lord, planet near cusp, aspects.
  - Moon in lagna: broad face, small nose, small hands/feet; restless — desire for changes of residence, job, city; never satisfied.
  - Moon in movable/common sign afflicted → gipsy life, frequent travel.
  - Aspects to Moon in lagna (Sun, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn) good/bad delineations — FLAG Western aspects. Useful embedded timing statements:
    - Results come in the mutual periods (e.g. Sun dasa–Moon bhukti or Moon dasa–Sun bhukti) "and whenever they form aspects by transit and progression" (FLAG progression).
    - Stellar day-timing: an event from Mercury–Moon is felt on Ashlesha star day (Mercury's star in Moon's sign) or Hasta star day (Moon's star in Mercury's sign), and when the Sun transits Hasta/Ashlesha (≈3–16 Aug, 26 Sep–9 Oct). **Early form of "star in sign of the other" timing — consistent with later RP/transit method.**
    - Saturn bad aspect to Moon in lagna: first marriage attempt may fail, second marriage later; first wife sickly; falls/injury; loss of mother or young relation.
    - Venus = worldly enjoyment (loka sukha), Jupiter = spiritual (para-loka sukha) → Hindus treat them as enemies.
    - Mars bad aspect (women): menstrual trouble; accidents, fire, boiling liquid, operation.
    - Nodes forming aspects give the results the lord of their sign would give. Uranus/Neptune have no dasa, so their aspects ignored (KSK).
  - **Lordship modifies whom the result applies to**: if the aspecting planet is the lagna lord → physique/health/success; lord of 2/6/10 (garbled, likely 2, 6, 10) → profession/finance; lord of 7 → partner; lord of 9 → father; lord of 3 → brother/sister. "Include in interpretation which house Moon is lord of." (Precursor to the KP rule that a planet signifies the houses it owns.)
- **How to overcome fate — Prarthana (vows)** (Delhi/Calcutta lecture): REJECT (remedies) — but KSK's own stance stated: vows give only mental solace; "Karma will overcome all your vows"; one gets results only if destined. Out of scope.
- **Prayers** (KSK): REJECT/out of scope (remedies), except astrological rule: **deity to pray to is shown by the 5th house** — occupants of 5, planets in their stars, lord of 5, planets in the star of the lord of 5, modified by conjoined/aspecting planets; changes with dasa/bhukti. (Same significator hierarchy as KP, applied to 5th.) List of 21 desires (longevity, health, accidents, income, debts, marriage, children, son/daughter, divorce, promotion, litigation, election, enemies, theft) — "whom to pray will follow".
- **Sun: when is he your secret foe? (Sun in 12th)** — delineation, partly traditional:
  - Sun in 12 when birth is sunrise to ~2 h after.
  - 12th house signifies: secret enemies, sorrow, suicide, isolation, hospitalisation, assassination, imprisonment, treason, loss, expenses, investment, life in a foreign place, change of place/environment, left eye.
  - Derived houses from 12th (useful for relatives module): profession of first younger sibling (10th from 3rd); long journey of mother & mother's father (9th from 4th); danger to children (8th from 5th); maternal uncle's partner and servants' wives (7th from 6th); wife's debts and disease (6th from 7th); father's mother (4th from 9th); elder brother's self-acquisition (2nd from 11th).
  - Sun in 12: ill-health at 36 (traditional, FLAG); bribery, dismissal, imprisonment. Pisces lagna (Sun L6): dismissed servants inform tax department. Aquarius lagna (Sun L7): partner informs.
  - Sun in 12 afflicted by Saturn → suicide tendency; with/afflicted by L6 → hospitalisation; with/afflicted by L8 → unnatural end (murder/accident) — **death mode: 12th + L8 = unnatural end (cf. later 8th/12th accident rule)**.
  - Sun in 12 with malefics in 2, 5, 9 → imprisonment in Sun's periods; benefics in 2/5/9 → detention/political prison; Jupiter+Sun in 12 → jail for civil offences; Venus+Sun → political offences. FLAG (traditional combinations).
  - Sun in 12 → leaving permanent residence, living in a foreign place, change of institution, loss of ancestral property, left eye trouble; jobs in medical, hospital, chemical, lab, jail, research; occult interest.
  - Relatives during Sun's period (derived): younger sibling prospers; mother long journey; mother's father ill; children accident/ill-health; maternal uncle marriage or legal dispute; servants' marriages; wife ailment/borrowing; business partner ill/borrows; father's mother eye disease; father disposes of property; elder brother gets service.
- **Moon: unsteady fortune (Moon in 2nd)**: 2nd = wealth/poverty, movable goods, money lent, speculation money, lawsuits, right eye, treasures, memory, speech, vision, bank balance, family, self-acquisition. Moon in 2: handsome, wealth, possible break in education, govt respect, witty speech (continues next chunk). Delineation only.

## MC_009 (Nov–Dec 1963) [detailed redo]
- **Moon in 2nd (contd.) — KEY early statement of the stellar method (KSK, Nov 1963)**:
  - Traditional: Moon owning a good house in 2 → wealth; owning evil house → loss; movable sign = short-lived gain/loss, fixed = slow, common = alternating. KSK: "these are general indications by various authors" — instead **take the star Moon occupies and the houses its star lord owns**.
  - **5-step procedure (core KP rule):** (1) note the house the planet occupies; (2) its nakshatra; (3) the star lord and which houses it owns; (4) read results as "lord of those houses occupying the house tenanted by the planet"; (5) modify the quality by the nature of the occupant and the star lord.
  - Example Gemini lagna, Moon in 2nd in Cancer:
    - Punarvasu 4 (Jupiter = L7, L10) → gain via wife/partner and profession.
    - Pushya (Saturn = L8, L9) → hard to collect debts, bad debts, death of an old family member, loss via insurance/legacy, family dispute (L8); also gain via foreign trade, shipping, export/import, science, publications, banking (L9). Strongest in Moon dasa – Saturn bhukti.
    - Ashlesha (Mercury = L1, L4) → gain by own effort; land, buildings, vehicles, estate; mother's gains given to native; esp. in Mercury bhukti.
  - Example Leo lagna, Moon (L12) in 2nd (Virgo): Uttaraphalguni (Sun L1) → earns by own effort, willing expenses; Hasta (Moon L12) → unexpected losses, expenses outweigh income; Chitra (Mars L4, L9) → spends on land, conveyance, education, long journey, parents — acquires them.
  - Example Aries lagna, Moon exalted in 2nd (Taurus): Krittika (Sun L5) → speculation/children/pleasure; Rohini (Moon L4) → fixed deposits, property, vehicles, mother; Mrigasira (Mars L1, L8) → own effort, builds house, legacy/insurance/provident fund.
  - **Timing: results of a planet are experienced in the bhukti/antara of that planet and of its star lord** (Mrigasira natives never get Moon dasa, so Moon's results come in Moon bhuktis).
  - Money lent (2nd): Moon in the star of lords of 1, 2, 3, 6, 10, 11 → debtors repay; gains via relatives of the star lord's houses. In star of lords of 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 12 → gain only if aspect from those lords is unfavourable; favourable aspect (sextile/trine) → loss. FLAG: odd aspect inversion; Western aspects; superseded later by 2/6/11 vs 5/8/12 significator rule. Note early list 1,2,3,6,10,11 = good for money recovery.
  - Announcement: KSK will publish "Krishnamurti Paddhathi" = "advanced stellar astrology combined with the Western system of aspects" (FLAG — 1963 definition includes aspects).
  - Moon in 2 = 8th from 7th → partner faces difficulties and drags native to court.
  - Aspects to Moon in 2 → speech qualities, eye (lord of 2 aspect), family size (Jupiter/Mars/Venus big; Saturn small). FLAG Western aspects.
- **Mars the independent (Mars in lagna)**: lagna = health, longevity, accidents, mind, form, success, rank. Derived from lagna: younger sibling's profits/friends (11th from 3), mother's reputation (10th from 4), children's long voyage/higher studies (9th from 5), danger to servants/maternal uncle/pets (8th from 6), father's speculation (5th from 9), elder brother's short journeys/publication (3rd from 11). Mars in lagna by sign Aries/Taurus/Gemini/Cancer delineations (character, health: fire, operation, fever; Gemini afflicted by Saturn/Uranus → death in air travel; Cancer afflicted → property loss by fire/storm/theft in mutual period of Mars and afflicting planet). FLAG sign delineation + Western aspects/Uranus.
- **No more doubts — King or Kubera (Balasundaram)**: chart cannot tell if one is born royal or beggar; can tell rise or fall relative to station. Time-twins cases: Samuel Hemmings & George III (b. 4-6-1738, both d. 29-1-1820); Edward VII & Walthamstow child (b. 9-11-1841, both d. 6-5-1910); William II & artisan; Prince George (IV) & chimney-sweep; two lecturers; two villagers. Principle: astrologer must know the client's station/background (results scale with status). App implication: show event nature, not absolute magnitude.
- **No more doubts — eclipsed (combust) planet**:
  - Orbs (Western): Sun 17°, Moon 12°, others 7°. FLAG.
  - Conjunction benefic+malefic: benefic's good reduced, malefic's evil mitigated (traditional).
  - Combustion: within 5° of Sun = really combust; within 10° ordinary; beyond 15° none. Nodes not combust. Planets with Moon = Samagamam (strength). Traditional table of results of combust lords of houses (L2 … L12) in their dasa/bhukti — FLAG traditional.
  - Modern Astrological Research Institute, Madras (KSK) verified charts: **eclipsed planets gave both good and bad results in their dasas, depending on houses**; e.g. Sagittarius lagna with Mercury combust had good prosperity in profession in Mercury dasa; Scorpio(?) lagna with Venus combust married in Venus dasa and enjoyed married life. **Conclusion: combustion does not make a planet malefic or deny its results** (it may reduce extent — cf. later note "combust planets give results to a lesser extent").
  - Western conjunction notes (Sun-Moon, Sun-Mars, Sun-Mercury, Sun-Saturn) FLAG.
- **Dec 1963 issue**: contents — Ayana chalana, Vakya vs Drik, which panchanga, bhukti, moles (REJECT), Mars in each star, Mars in each sign, Cancer, Sun period, transits, monthly forecasts.
- **Ayana chalana (L. Narayana Rao)**: precession explained; luni-solar 54"/yr, planetary −3.75", net ~50.25"/yr (avg 50.23"); Surya Siddhanta 54"/yr, Graha Laghava 1'/yr; cycle ~25,800 yr; libration theory (not relevant now). Methods to find ayanamsa: timed events via dasa; Nadi dasa balances (flawed); his proposal: compare Sun's mean positions (modern vs Surya Siddhanta) at perihelion → 1963 April ayanamsa commonly 23°19'(modern) vs SS 21°57'36". Contributor view; KSK's own ayanamsa defined elsewhere (see Readers notes). For app: use KSK ayanamsa.
- **Vakya and Drik (Narayana Rao)**: Vakya (Vararuchi's ready-made tables from Surya Siddhanta) vs Drik (observation-verified, modern almanac − ayanamsa). Moon differences up to 1 ghatika in tithi/nakshatra; Mars/Mercury large disparity. **Use Drik (observed) positions** — supports Swiss Ephemeris.
- **Which panchanga to use (KSK)**: ancient astronomers said calculations must be revised to agree with observation (continues next chunk).

## MC_010 (Dec 1963) [detailed redo]
- **Which panchanga (KSK, contd.)**: classical authors (Uttara Kalamrita, Phaladeepika) say positions must agree with observation (Drik). Test method: compare predicted Moon rising degree / eclipse timings under Vakya vs Drik → Drik correct. Kanchi Sankaracharya's judges decided on 16-12-1937 that Drik alone be followed. Slogan: **"Follow Drik and Drik alone."** → APP: use modern ephemeris (Swiss Ephemeris), not Vakya.
- **Swa-bhukti question (KSK)**: querent running Saturn dasa – Venus bhukti; Saturn–Saturn was good (salary, status rose). A student had threatened that a planet doing good in its own bhukti will do harm later (traditional dictum) — KSK rejects; advice: get chart correctly cast, sub-divide bhukti into antaras, check diary events against antaras. Dates given: Saturn dasa starting 10-3-1956 → Saturn–Saturn 10-3-1956 to ~13-3-1959; Saturn-Saturn antaras: Sat 10-3-56→1-9-56, Merc →4-2-57, Ketu →8-4-57, Venus →8-10-57, Sun →2-12-57 …; later bhuktis: Venus 1-1-63→1-3-66, Sun →13-2-67, Moon →13-9-68, Mars →22-10-69, Rahu →28-8-72, Jupiter →10-3-75. (Usable as a dasa-calculation test: Saturn bhukti 3y 0m 3d; Saturn dasa from 10-3-1956 ends 10-3-1975.) Statement: "Thorough study of dasa system, progression and transit needed" (FLAG progression). KP chapter to be serialised from 1964. Swa-bhukti results by sages — duplicates Readers. **Rule: no blanket rule that own-bhukti good ⇒ later bad; judge by significations.**
- **Moles and their meaning**: REJECT (out of scope).
- **Mars in each star (Aswini … Arudra)**: delineations; framework stated by KSK: a planet gives results (a) by nature, (b) by lordship, (c) modified by sign, (d) by constellation, (e) by conjunctions, (f) by aspects. Star-specific notes: Mars in Bharani → results especially in Mars dasa – Venus bhukti (star lord's bhukti). Rest is character/profession description (FLAG as generic delineation; not timing rules).
- **Mars in each sign (Leo, Virgo, Libra)**: delineation with Western aspects (FLAG). Notables: Mars+node in Leo in 12th → imprisonment/hospitalisation in joint periods; Mars afflicted by Venus → late marriage/difficulty; Uranus square Mars → unlucky (FLAG).
- **Cancer (Arudhra)**: sun-sign delineation (Sayana 21 Jun–23 Jul; Nirayana 17 Jul–16 Aug). Traditional dasa-sequence remarks: Cancer lagna natives run dasas of lords of 6/9 (Jupiter), 8 (Saturn), 12 (Mercury) early — Punarvasu 4/Pushya births have trials in first half of life; Ashlesha births better; Moon in Ketu/Venus stars good start. FLAG (generic lordship reading; ignores star/sub). Life-phase ages (36–43, 46, 33) traditional — REJECT.
- **Are you running Sun period? (K. Ganapathy)**:
  - Sun dasa bhukti lengths: Sun 3m18d, Moon 6m, Mars 4m6d, Rahu 10m24d, Jupiter 9m18d, Saturn 11m12d, Mercury 10m6d, Ketu 4m6d, Venus 1y. (Standard Vimshottari — test data.)
  - General Sun-period delineation (fame, govt favour, permanency, promotion in Jupiter/Mercury/Venus bhuktis). Saturn aspect = delays; obstructing officer is "Saturn's avatar" (born Saturday / Saturn star / Capricorn-Aquarius lagna); when Saturn bhukti ends he is transferred/retires/dies. FLAG (folk).
  - **Health rule (KP-style significator logic)**: a bhukti lord causes illness if it is lord of 6, conjoined with L6, in the star of L6, in the star of the occupant of the 6th, or in 6/8/12 from the dasa lord (Sun). Disease type by planet: Moon — inflammatory/epidemic; Mars — acute short illness, measles, pox, accidents, burns, fever; Mercury — nervous/mental, liver, gas; Jupiter — excess, obesity, blood disorders; Venus — throat, eyes, generative organs (esp. in 2/5/7/9), venereal; Sun+Venus — uterus/ovary removal, fibroid; Saturn — bones, fractures, falls, rheumatism, chills; Sun+Saturn+Mars — amputation; **Rahu/Ketu — ailments of the lord of the star they occupy; harmful only if in 6, in star of L6, or with L6.** (Useful for health module; note 6/8/12-from-dasa-lord rule is contributor/traditional.)
  - Finance in Sun period: Mars connection → lavish spending; Jupiter → prestige expenses; Saturn/Ketu → waste.
  - **Profession by planets connected with houses 2, 6, 10** (long combination list): e.g. Sun = Government; Sun+Mars = defence, military surgeon; Sun+Mars+Jupiter = police/law; Sun+Mars+Saturn by sign element: fiery = war front, earthy = HQ (if Jupiter aspects), airy = air force, watery = navy; Sun+Mercury = literature, science, banking, accountancy, posts, editor, broker, typist; Sun+Mercury+Jupiter+Venus+Mars = income-tax officer, sessions judge, IAS; Sun+Moon+Venus = dairy; Sun+Moon+Saturn+Venus in 2/6/10 = fishery, petrol, mine; Sun+Mars+Rahu+Saturn in 2 & 12 = prisoner; in 6/10 = executioner; Sun+Mercury+Mars+Uranus = radio (FLAG Uranus). "Planets need not be conjoined — any connection with 2, 6, 10." **APP: profession nature = planets signifying 2, 6, 10 (combination table — contributor K. Ganapathy, list to be cross-checked with KSK's later profession articles).**

- **NMD: Results of conjoined planets (KSK)** — CORE RULES:
  - (A) Same sign, different bhavas: each planet gives results of the bhava it occupies (if no planet in its stars); not a blend ("rose, apple, book in one plate").
  - (B) Same bhava: each gives that bhava's results if none in its stars; but the **primary/most important result is what the star lord signifies by its occupation**; bhava occupation is additional.
  - (C) Same constellation: give the results mainly of the star lord (house occupied by the star lord); plus own bhava if no planet in its star. This is "sthula"; **"sukshma" results only from the sub lord.**
  - **Same star, different subs → can give diametrically opposite results.** Example: star lord in 5th (childbirth); planet in sub of a 2/11 significator (text says "2 or 3 or 11" — likely typo for 2, 5, 11) → child birth; another planet in same star but sub of significator of 1, 4, 10 (detrimental to 5th — 12th from 2, 5, 11) → no child; maybe separation from a child already born. Another: star lord in 10, one planet in sub of 12th significator (loss of job / leave) vs. other in 11th-significator sub (gain/promotion) — (continues next chunk).
  - **Negating houses for children: 1, 4, 10** (12th from 2, 5, 11). APP: sub lord decides yes/no.

  - Don't predict failure because planets are in 6/8/12. (1) Ignore the "evil sign"; (2) planets in 6/8/12 may be in the stars of occupants/owners of 9, 10, 11 → progress, prosperity, progeny, profit, partnership — predict boldly.
  - Example: Capricorn lagna; Ketu in Libra in 10th; Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury in 12th (Sagittarius) in Moola (Ketu) and Purvashada (Venus); Venus in 11th bhava (Venus L3? "lord of 3 and 10" — for Capricorn Venus = L5 & L10; text garbled) → results of 10th (via Ketu) and 11th (via Venus) → most advantageous.

- **NMD: Why Moon sign is taken (if lagna afflicted)? (KSK)** — IMPORTANT, partly resolves earlier FLAG:
  - Affliction definition: a house/planet is afflicted if conjoined with a natural malefic (Mars, Saturn, Rahu, Ketu) **in the same sign** (anywhere in the sign, even if in 12th bhava behind the cusp — "cobra in the room"); a house is weakened if its lord is in 6/8/12 from the sign it owns, or the lord of 6/8/12 from that sign occupies it. Malefic in adjacent sign near the cusp (29° prev sign) does NOT afflict ("guard van" analogy) — **sign-based, because lord of a bhava = lord of the sign of its cusp**.
  - Views: some take lagna until age 32 then Moon sign; some always lagna; some take unafflicted Moon sign if lagna afflicted; if both afflicted take lagna.
  - **KSK research: (a) natal — take the lagna alone if it is unafflicted, or if Moon sign is also afflicted; (b) where lagna is afflicted and Moon sign is not, take only the Moon sign.** "We follow a method only when it proves universally correct on past events." **Horary: always take the lagna** (affliction of horary lagna indicates adverse health/longevity/success).
  - Planets in own sign = strong, not auspicious; malefics remain malefics; **ownership of 9, 10, 11 makes a planet give agreeable results**. Mars in Aries strengthens houses with cusps in Aries; weak for Scorpio-cusp houses (6th from it).
  - **Students' research rule: a malefic in the lagna sign in the star of the occupant or owner of 9, 10, 11 becomes a benefic** (gives results of its star lord) → lagna then not afflicted.
  - KP weakening: a planet is weakened if in the star of the occupant/owner of 6, 8, 12, or conjoined with a planet in such a star. House weakened if unoccupied and its lord in 6/8/12 from its sign, or conjoined with lord of 6/8/12.
  - FLAG/CONTRADICTION: Moon-sign-as-lagna rule for natal still endorsed here (Jan 1968) — conflicts with later KSK (cusps only). App: decide; default to lagna/cusps, flag optional Moon-chart mode. See "Love marriage" case below where RPs decided between Libra lagna and Cancer Moon sign.

Twins/born-minutes-apart charts are the research test (same rasi, navamsa, bhava chalit — only cusps/subs differ). - **Astrology—a science (S. N. Mishra's Madras speech, reported by K. Ganapathi)** — summary of KP as practised:
  1. Star divided into 9 unequal subs (Vimshottari proportions), each sub into 9 sub-subs → 81 parts per 13°20'; verified on many twins.
  2. Western (Placidus) house division — verified.
  3. Specific house groups for each matter + significator order.
  4. Sign = strength; planet = source by occupation/ownership; star lord = nature; **sub lord = final authority whether the matter will be fulfilled.**
  5. Timing: transit of luminaries + dasa and bhukti lords; RPs at judgment a good guide.
  6–7. **KSK ayanamsa "very correct"; use Raphael's ephemeris, Raphael's Tables of Houses and KSK ayanamsa.** Many astrologers casting the same time get one chart.
  8. KP includes Western aspects (FLAG, 1968).
  9. Nodes very important; tenants stronger than owners; planet in a star stronger than the star lord.
  10. No house/planet wholly good or evil — good for native, bad for wife etc. (derived houses).
  11. Exaltation etc. given undue importance traditionally; in KP stellar position reigns supreme.
  12. Rectification pinpoints one time; verified on known births.

- **Role of sub-sub (KSK)** — CORE:
  - Strength from nirayana sign position (regardless of degree within the sign).

  - **Two planets in close conjunction in the same sign, star and sub: one gives, the other doesn't → the sub-sub decides which.** Research: people born 14–27 April in Aswini (Sun & Moon near conjunction, Ketu dasa at birth): Venus dasa studies & service; Sun dasa good report & promotion; Moon dasa (10 yrs) always missed promotion — Sun was in sub-sub of an 11th-house significator, Moon was not.
  - **Sub-sub to pin-point day/hour/minute via transits**: Saturn ~2.5 yrs/sign, ~13 months/star, a few weeks per sub → sub-sub for the day. Moon ~24 h/star, ~3 h/sub → sub-sub for exact time. Lagna ~2 h/sign, ~32 min/star, ~5–8 min/sub → sub-sub for exact minute.
- **Dina porutham / nakshatra porutham (KSK)**: traditional counting from bride's star to groom's (Kalaprakasika "sthree janma thaaram arabhya"; Jataka Chandrika "vadhoo nakshatram arabhya"). KSK: couples with dina porutham still divorce, die early, quarrel → porutham does not assure harmony. **KP method of comparison is "absolutely different"** (continues next chunk). A capable astrologer must predict (a) whether married, (b) when, (c) how married life will be, (d) its duration.
